## 15 · ADDED AT v1.9 — 22-Sep to 25-Sep-2026 (sessions S281 and S283)

*Every fact here was read from an installer's own output, the medical PC's heartbeat and watcher log in Drive, the live pages in the owner's browser, or the 25-Sep 01:05 server database. Kits named are in `deploy_kits\` in the repository; their pins are in the Register.*

### 15.1 · Marg's exports travel as TEXT (D615)
- **Why:** Microsoft Office broke on the medical PC (24-Sep), so Marg's Excel export failed, and nothing in the estate read Marg's one-click **text** export (`C:\Users\Public\MARG\<id>\report.txt`) — F-619.
- **How it works now:** `D:\SendToClinic\marg_txt.py` on the medical PC turns a Marg text export into **the same `.XLS` Marg's Excel export is** (BIFF8, standard library only, deterministic). Every reader downstream — the server's door, the router, the spine — sees an ordinary Marg `.XLS`; nothing on the VPS or manojz changed for it. `marg_watch.py` takes the text file, converts it, captures the `.XLS`, and keeps the text in `_captured_txt\`.
- **What it reads:**
  - **the bill-wise sale with item detail** — S389, parity-proved against Marg's own 22-Sep `.XLS` (27 bills identical in every field, grand total to the paisa; S389.2 fixed two-digit line numbers), LIVE since 20:02 24-Sep (S390, switch `MARG_TXT_LIVE.txt`);
  - **the whole-stores closing stock** — S397, proved 14/14 against Marg's 23-Sep Excel and the 24-Sep sales; LIVE 14:51 IST 25-Sep on the owner's OK. The 24-Sep closing stock (exported 07:36, refused by the old reader) was taken at 14:51:41 and the server's check finished at 15:01 — **378 items** (Shavez's page, `https://followup.dr-manoj.in/finance/reports/aaj`: "Aaj ka kaam poora").
- **The rule for Shavez:** the bill-wise sale is exported **with item detail**. The SUMMARY layout has no item lines and no GRAND TOTAL; it is refused, correctly, and kept with the reason.
- **Excel stays the fallback.** Both routes land in the same place.
- **Pins (medical PC):** `marg_watch.py` 81145aa7 (S397) · `marg_txt.py` 38d85298 (S397) · Drive `_kit\KIT_MANIFEST.txt` 8230562e (the CRLF form; the repository copy is stored LF — the CRLF note in the S281 close).

### 15.2 · The agent runs whichever Windows account is signed in (F-618)
- Until 24-Sep the agent (capture + push + offsite backup) started only from **SET's own** start-up folder; when SET signed out and the staff account `user` was used, capture stopped silently.
- `D:\SendToClinic\agent_guard.py` (S387) — **one agent for the whole machine**: a lock on D:, a standby that takes over when the running session signs out, and it stands aside for the old launcher. `START_AGENT.cmd` is each account's start-up entry; SET's was replaced at S387 (SET has no administrator rights, so it is per-account). `ENABLE_AGENT_THIS_ACCOUNT.bat` (S388) was run once in `user` at 15:41 24-Sep. S386 was refused by its own on-machine proof and is frozen.
- `marg_watch.py` also sweeps the **top level of the pen drive `E:\`** for `.xls/.xlsx/.pdf` written in the last 24 hours (S381, F-617), because Marg once saved an export there.

### 15.3 · What the medical PC tells Drive (read these first when "an export has not come")
`Clinic Data Archive\FromMedical\` — reachable with the Drive connector even when manojz is off:
- `heartbeat.txt` / `heartbeat.json` — every ~10 minutes: watcher alive, captures today, the md5 of every kit file installed vs in `_kit`, backups, disk;
- `marg_watch_log.txt` — the watcher's own log tail (S395; before that its messages went nowhere, F-620);
- `marg_text_census.txt` — every recent `.txt` where an export could land, with its verdict (S396; never a patient's name);
- `refused_text\` — any text export refused, with a `.why.txt` (S396.1).
Installing on the medical PC is a Drive operation: a file placed at the top of `ToMedical\_kit\` under a name the agent knows is installed only if its md5 matches `KIT_MANIFEST.txt`; a subfolder is inert (that is how S397 was pre-staged).

### 15.4 · A machine-filed day follows Marg until it is approved (D602, S367)
`day_resync.py` pass 0: an unapproved, autofiled day whose Marg bills later add up differently is re-set to Marg's net (cash = net − the bank's UPI), with an audit row. An approved day that differs is **named**, never touched. The day panel says how each day was filed (which export, what time, nobody typed it). 04-Sep was corrected visibly to Marg's 18 bills (23,875) under D602.

### 15.5 · The approvals page is ONE tree (D603, S368)
`https://followup.dr-manoj.in/finance/approvals` — *Needs you* in words at the top, then Days, Cash, Bank, Returns, Month, and the audit material as a collapsed **Checks** section on the same page. The old page stays one link away (`/finance/approvals/old`). Each day opens into the S365 day panel: Sale → bills → medicines, returns named, UPI (the bank's), **Without cash** (home / procedure medicine), Cash received = sale − returns − UPI − without cash, where the cash went.

### 15.6 · Two bank accounts (D604, S375/S377/S378)
- **ICICI Sanjeevni** collects the card/QR (POS) settlements; its balance is counted from the bank's own closing balance on 31-Aug (1,50,263.42), plus settlements since, minus recorded transfers (S377 — before it, the count started from zero and wrongly refused a real 40,000 transfer, F-615).
- **Yes Bank Sanjeevni** takes the cash deposits, pays the suppliers by **one bulk NEFT** a month (the month's purchases, paid in the next month, latest by its third week) and carries the POS rental + GST.
- Either account may transfer to its own HUF savings. **A transfer is never income.**
- The Bank section shows both balances boldly (S378), each saying how it was made and that anything the bank took since the last statement is not in it until the next statement. The owner records deposits and transfers himself on the page.

### 15.7 · The month's payment papers are one pack (D605, S380)
One button prints the covering letter (portrait), the annexure / NEFT advice (landscape) and the payment sheet (portrait), each on its own sheet, the last two with "For SANJEEVNI MEDICOS / Authorized Signatory". The pay page's own print now gives the annexure alone, with no blank pages after it (F-616). A purchase bill marked Wrong clears itself when Marg's re-export agrees (S371, F-614), so a month can be finalised.

### 15.8 · Home and procedure medicine — how the page finds them (F-632, S399)
- Darpan bills these to a **label**, not a person: `HOME MEDICINE`, `PROSIJER <name>`. The words live in the setting table (`noncash.home_words` = `HOME MEDI`; `noncash.proc_words` = `PROSIJ, PROSEJ, PROCIJ, PROCED, PROSED, PRUSIJ`) — a new spelling is a data edit, never code.
- `day_resync.py` pass 2 turns each label bill into one `day_noncash_bill` row, so the day's cash is not read high by it; a credit note on such a bill is goods back, not cash short (+ one cash adjustment, D594).
- **The gap the owner found (25-Sep):** A003806 of 23-Sep, `PROSIJER <patient>`, Rs 195 — the ingest's lookup ladder found the patient and filed the bill against them, where no customer text is kept, and pass 2 looked **only** in the review queue. 23-Sep read 11,799 instead of 11,604.
- **The repair, S399 (built, publish-ready):** pass 2 also reads the label words in the bill text the ingest keeps for every bill it laddered (`identity_resolution`) or name-checked (`identity_dispute`); an approved day holding a label bill with no deduction is named, never touched. Proved on a scratch copy of the 25-Sep database (10 checks, RED against S367).
- **The sweep, every Marg bill 17-Aug → 24-Sep:** the only label bills without a deduction are A003806 (above) and three on approved August days — 17-Aug A003014 HOME MEDICINE 1,076, and 18-Aug A003043 / A003044 at Rs 1 each. August's cash was proved to the rupee against the physical counts (S361), so they are named and left. Every other check — filed net vs Marg, UPI vs the bank, every deduction vs its Marg bill — agrees on every day.

### 15.9 · Rulings of 22–25-Sep
D602 (04-Sep is Marg's 18 bills; a machine-filed day follows Marg until approved) · D603 (the approvals page is one tree) · D604 (two accounts; a transfer is never income; the bulk NEFT pays the month in the next month) · D605 (the payment pack) · D615 (Marg's exports travel as text; Excel the fallback; the sale always with item detail).

### 15.10 · Faults of the week
F-613 (a filed day never refreshed by a later export) · F-614 (a Wrong mark that could not be cleared) · F-615 (ICICI counted from zero) · F-616 (six printed pages, five blank) · F-617 (an export on the pen drive's root) · F-618 (the agent tied to one account) · F-619 (nothing read Marg's text export) · F-620 (the watcher's messages went nowhere) · F-632 (a laddered label bill never reached pass 2).

## 15A · Added at v1.10 — the eight days of S283 (25-Sep → 02-Oct-2026)

*Numbered 15A because it follows §15. Where it and an earlier section disagree, this is the later reading and wins. Every file md5 is in the Register v5.127 §S283, each read in the 02-Oct 01:35 bundle.*

### 15A.1 · How a pharmacy build is made now (D617, D648, F-679)

- **The chat writes the WHAT; Claude Code builds it.** A brief goes into `claude_code_briefs\S###_NAME.md`. The owner pastes one line. Claude Code reads `CLAUDE.md`, builds in scratch, walks the build on copies of the live databases, backs up, installs, reads every md5 back, restores itself on a red check, publishes, and writes `REPORT_S###.md`.
- **Every duty has a door.** `claude_code_briefs\DUTY_MAP.md` and `.json` list each person's duties (42 at S444), the state that says a duty is due, and the screen that shows it. A kit that adds or moves a duty updates the map. A kit is done only when its walk signs in as each affected login and finds every pending duty on that login's home.
- **The brief is confirmed by its hash where it lands** before the paste line is given, and a report is read against the brief's section list. S444 was built from an earlier text of its brief because this was not done (F-679).

### 15A.2 · The day is checked by Bhati (D616 — S399, S400, S402)

- **The tile *Medical sale check*** (`sale_check.py`, `sale_check.html`) shows only the days not yet approved.
- He holds Marg's sale sheet and Darpan's copy against the system and marks *Sahi hai* or *Galti hai*: bill and problem by drop-down, typing only for *other*.
- He enters only the amount Darpan handed (to Dr Bhawna by default). Red flags show where Darpan did not log, or the amount differs. The day's sale returns sit in one collapsible block.
- He cannot see the month, the pool, the banks or the returns desk. The server enforces it. The owner remains the approver.
- **A procedure-medicine bill attached to a patient is deducted from the day's cash** (F-632): `day_resync` pass 2 reads the bill text from the review queue, `identity_resolution` and `identity_dispute`.

### 15A.3 · Buying without the paper sheet (D618, D626, D623 — S403, S410, S414, S417, S407)

- **Purchase orders** (`porders.py`, `porders.html`): shortages from keep-in-stock numbers, a one-tap WhatsApp order, arrival taps (*Aa gaya / Kam aaya / Supplier ke paas nahi*), auto-clear when the Marg purchase arrives, the bill's scan linked. Stock is shown in the order's own unit.
- **The buying rules are settings** (`order_rules.py`; the values the owner set live in `spine/order_rules.json`):
  - cadence from the shop's own rhythm (weekly, fortnightly or monthly);
  - fixed order days — weekly Monday; Kedar Monday and Friday, Thursday open for Kedar, Sunday blocked;
  - lead 1 day, learned per supplier; safety 3;
  - cover caps 14 / 21 / 45 days;
  - a Kedar review to weekly four weeks after go-live.
- The owner edits them on the approvals page; the block patches in place (F-635). The team's day on *Kal ka hisaab* is built from them.
- **NEFT done is one owner tap.** Supplier messages with the account number and IFSC are queued on the server (`supplier_msg.py`) and sent by the reception phone's macro; the numbers never enter the repository. **18 messages queued since 26-Sep have not left the phone** — open (15A.9).

### 15A.4 · Bills on paper: lanes, the matcher, *Scan ka kaam* (D625, D640 — S409, S439, S440; the parent's S441)

- **Whoever holds the paper scans it**, in the asset app's intake: five lanes, the login's own default, a duplicate guard, re-lane in one tap. The intake opens on the camera. Since the parent's S441 each photo is a page with one Save, and the shared Reception login asks who is working.
- **The scans are read by Sarvam Document AI** (the shared helper under `/root/shared`). Our code only matches its reading to Marg's bills.
- **The matcher** (`purchase_app.py`) is built on the forms OCR actually writes: the bill number's digit tail with the amount, vendor similarity with learned spellings (`purchase_scan_alias`), the buyer read as vendor, years off ignored. It re-matches at every push and nightly. State is kept in `purchase_scan_state` and `purchase_scan_link`.
- ***Scan ka kaam*** on Purchase orders gives reception one tap per line: *Scan karo · Yahi bill hai? · Supplier chuno · Amount milao · Marg ka intezaar · Galat lane*, with the scan's picture on each line. Every scan page has a sticky ← BACK and a floating ↑.
- **Before S441, 65 of 102 scans were made under the shared Reception login**, so who scanned them is unknown.

### 15A.5 · Returns, the bank SMS, the month-end packs (D622, D621, D624 — S406, S405, S439, S408, S411, S412)

- **Returns are two kinds** (`returns_kinds.py`): counter returns against the owner's non-cash returns, each as a percentage of counter sales. Rounding noise is not a finding.
- **The bank-SMS door** (`bank_sms.py`) keeps a masked note of every post it refuses, and reads the SMS the way the macro sends it — as the query string's key (F-660). Ten ICICI settlement SMS were recovered from the web log, each equal to the bank's MPR.
- **The month-end packs are the parent's**, built here: the statement shelf (`stmt_shelf.py`), the ICICI reader, the server unlocking Yes Bank's statements, Shavez's checklist (`packs_checklist.html`), the accountant's pack, Amir's pack.

### 15A.6 · The count of 06-Sep, closed (D631 … D633, D637, D638, D620 — S404, S418 … S437)

- **The owner's Loss desk is four piles, one tap each** (`loss_piles.py`): *Accept back · Write off · Pursue · Recount*. The piles are computed once per count and stored. Hub, desk and report read one total.
- **The rules:**
  - piles by turnover — an allowance as a percentage of the item's own sales since the last count;
  - consumption only from the owner's named list;
  - *Owner's use* is a recorded non-loss group;
  - old unpriced unmoved stock is written off and is never a loss;
  - the leakage budget is a percentage of sales, spread over the window the loss accrued in;
  - quantities are always strips + tabs or pcs (`qty_words.py`).
- **Count #1 was closed by the owner on 27-Sep:** 136 lines written off, Rs 64,679 at MRP.
- **Stock watch** (`stock_watch.py`, the 06:30 job): full counts on Sundays every two months; spot counts three mornings a week chosen by score; Darpan's *Kam mila* starts a trace from the last matched point.
- **Darpan's *Stock milaan*** (`stockmatch.py`): one answer per orthotic line.
- **The count statement** (`stock_statement.py`) lists the count section by section; orthotics are their own section in every count document.
- **A rename in Marg is followed by the system** (`item_alias.py`, applied at every import).
- **The count's 37 Marg vouchers are not yet entered** (28 ISSUE, 9 RECEIVE; 7 orthotic with 30 lines; 30 medicine or consumable). The 22 orthotic renames and live orthotic ordering wait on the orthotic ones.
- **Held by the owner:** the count's presentation rebuilt once from the mock he approved (D639, S438_COUNT_BOOK).

### 15A.7 · Amir's day after S444 (D647, D648 — live 01-Oct 22:08 IST)

- **One sign-in name.** `clinic_sso.py` lower-cases and trims the name when the session is made and when it is read, old sessions included. Before, "Amir" got a session no grant matched (F-669).
- **His login opens on *Amir ka kaam***, once per sign-in (`tile_grants.json` `users.amir.home`); his tiles are under *Sab tiles*. Every staff page says who is signed in. A login with no work sees a Hindi line and its attendance only.
- **A card on every step:** *Stock voucher baaki: 7 orthotic, 30 dawa — kholiye*. It opens his stock board, which has BACK to his day. Step 6 is *Marg sudhar*.
- **Each bill shows Marg's amount beside the paper's** (the scan's total, or the amount reception typed): *mil gaya*, *farq Rs X*, or *scan nahi hua*.
- **The seventh answer, *Meri entry galat thi — Marg mein theek kar di*,** raises no claim and clears itself when an export shows Marg equal to the paper. KEDAR 195 was settled this way by the owner's ruling.
- **The salt list cannot be forgotten silently:** a red card on his steps 6 and 7 while a tick is newer than the last verified list; Shavez's reports row shows the days owed.
- **The owner's Needs-you lines appear by themselves** and leave when fixed. Settings: `amir.salt_owed_days` 2, `amir.self_exports` 2, `amir.claim_open_days` 7, `amir.voucher_visits` 2.
  - the salt list late;
  - an own correction not seen in Marg after 2 exports;
  - a claim for Darpan open 7 days;
  - the vouchers not opened in 2 of his visits;
  - a duty with no door;
  - a report the server refused.

### 15A.8 · Decided, written, not yet built — S446_AMIR_STAGES_BILLS (D649, D650)

- **The count as gated stages:** orthotic vouchers → the server's proof on his closing-stock export → the 22 renames → verified → medicine vouchers, 5 per visit, each batch verified.
- **His monthly packs chosen by state**, not by "last month" (F-673).
- **The scanned medicine bills as files** for Marg's own digital entry. Marg's entry is final and, for two months, the judge of Sarvam's reading.
- **`marg_txt` learns Marg's `***`**, and a refusal on the medical PC reaches the owner (F-674).
- **Doors for the pharmacy duties the map found without one**, and lists by state, not by month (F-680).

### 15A.9 · Open at this close

- The 18 supplier messages not leaving the reception phone since 26-Sep.
- The spine rungs 4a–4f: nothing a person sees reads the spine except item lookup on the orders page (`S283_SPINE_READINESS_27SEP.md`).
- The *Medicine bills* scan home, after the staff's word on the walkthrough mock.
- The Marg purchase import, after one photo of Marg's import screen.
- 44 bundle files under `/root/finance` and `/root/marg_ingest` with no full-hash pin row (`S283_CLOSE_REPORT.md`).

## 15B · Added at v1.11 — 02 → 04-Oct-2026 (the S283 POST-CLOSE and session 285)

*Where §15B and an earlier section disagree, §15B is the later reading and wins. Read from Claude Code's reports S446, S452, S454 (parts 1 … 5, P4C) and S470, the live pages walked on 02, 03 and 04-Oct, the 04-Oct 01:35 bundle, and the counter day of 04-Oct as it happened.*

### 15B.1 · Amir's day — the count staged, the panel fixed (S446, S452; 02-Oct)

- **The count is three gated stages (D649).** A: the 7 orthotic vouchers → proved by a closing-stock export → B: the 22 orthotic renames → proved by Marg's own export → C: the medicine and consumable vouchers, 12 a visit (`amir.vouchers_per_visit`), the next lot on his next visit, proved or not. His page shows the stage he is in; the owner's view shows all 37. Din band is never blocked by a voucher.
- **The proof** (`stock_app._proof_state`): Marg's movement between the last export before the first voucher tick and the first export after the last tick, against the voucher lines' `change` sums; it needs the day's expected feed (made from the sale export), so a round ticked on day D is proved on D+1 morning. The renames open only on `done`. **Seen 04-Oct:** all 7 orthotic vouchers ticked 09:46–10:02, Marg vouchers 1–4; the closing stock exported 10:22; the proof waits for the 04-10 feed. One voucher line voided by the owner's VPS line (ANKLE BINDER BAMBOO L, round 4 ISSUE batch 1: Marg held 2 and voucher 1 brought it to 0); the void is a reason appended, `change` 0, `marg_to = marg_from`.
- **Bills for Marg's digital entry:** only scans with no likely Marg bill (F-686), named with the stamp, Amir and the owner only; the Sarvam reading is a trial until 02-Dec (D650). The phone-setup page is the owner's and a checker's, every showing audited (F-687). Step 7 and the board in Roman Hindi; the rate duty on his card (Marg's own rate or MRP clears it); NEFT shown to him only once confirmed, as one line and the paid sheet (`packs.py`, one edit).
- **The salts** (`purchase_salt_task`): sections rename / create / change / cleanup / **waiting**; Amir's sheet excludes *waiting* — which is the owner's section 4 — and `_marg_says` never says *done* for it. **F-726:** an answered section-4 row therefore never reaches Amir until the next kit moves it to *change*. The Excel salt-list reader took the shop's header line as a row (*SANJEEVNI MEDICOS* as JARDIANCE's salt). Three items ticked 01-Oct stayed *not in Marg's list* for Marg's own spellings (PAROXITENE CR 12.5; LAXATIVE SYP) until the owner corrected the names in Marg on 04-Oct.

### 15B.2 · The purchase flow as it is now (S454, 03 → 04-Oct; D662, D663, D665 … D669)

- **The order comes from Darpan's Marg order sheet (D666).** *Pending Orders (Purchase)*, saved as text under Marg's one default name on the medical PC; `marg_txt.py` (ed17bb76) recognises it by its title (kind ORDER) and pushes it; `order_sheet.py` loads it (`order.source = marg_sheet`); the system's own list runs silently beside it. The road proved itself on 04-Oct 09:48 (Darpan's first text, c602a5ae, loaded by itself). Old pending lines on the sheet are tagged, not dropped (D669); past `order.old_show_days` they sit on the owner's card only.
- **Reception's screen (`/finance/porders`) is one task at a time (D665):** *Aaj ka kaam* — order by phone (*Call karo* with the number shown; *Order ho gaya* ticked on the list; an unticked order closes on its bill's scan, D668), *WhatsApp all* from the reception mobile, the printed A4 sheet with the whole open order and tick columns, *Maal aaya?* with everything assumed received and the exceptions tapped, the bill scan offered and optional (a scan counts as arrival, D668), the scan list five at a time by supplier, one question per screen, *"Ek scan mein ek hi bill."* under both scan buttons (F-712), *"Kya yeh dawa ka bill hai?"* for a paper with no number and no amount (F-695). September is parked behind its own link (`purchase.parked_months`); the old page is `?old=1` with the owner's cards and settings. One reminder a day at 17:00.
- **The register (D662).** Every Marg bill of the month on the Scan links page with its paper, in exactly one state — Verified / Has its scan / Amount differs / No scan / Entered twice / Accepted without paper — by one set of pairing rules shared with the matcher, the questions and the Sarvam counter (F-690). Verified on supplier, bill number, date, total; item lines by Marg's arithmetic and learnt names; Marg stands on a differing date or number (D650). The month reads *N of N*; Shavez's morning line and the owner's Needs-you count from `purchase.register_from` (01-Oct). September's register at the close: 80 bills = 53 verified, 8 with their scan, 6 amount differs, 12 no scan, 1 entered twice.
- **Amir's soft start:** `purchase.entry_mode = paper` (default) or `both`; `digital` refused; nothing new reaches him. **Vendor payments** — the page, the letter, the annexure, the pack — for `supplier_msg.senders` (the owner, Shavez) only (D663).
- **The shelf figure (D667, `shelf_figure.py`):** the 06-Sep count's quantities + purchases − sales + returns since (`order.stock_basis = count`); Marg's daily closing kept beside it; a gap that changes sends the item to the spot-count list. It reads `stock_watch.Spine` still (the next kit moves it onto `spine_read`). A credit note is never a sale in any reader (F-713).
- **The reception phone** (MacroDroid *Sanjeevni Whatsapp* on WhatsApp Business, set up 03-Oct with the owner): fetches `/finance/api/supplier-msg/next` every minute while unlocked and lit, opens `wa.me`, presses Send, posts *done* — **blind (F-708):** it says *sent* whatever happened; the queue has no in-flight state (F-702). `order.phone_alive_min` 720 (a dark phone does not ask; that is normal). The owner's *Reception phone* card: the phone's state, a test message to his own number, never the key. All 18 August NEFT messages went 03-Oct (D670: payment messages go as today; nothing is held).
- **The Items check (P5, the owner only):** September's bills whose item lines add up (57 of 81); supplier item names learnt from pairings (36 at the close; the first learnt name wins and cannot be struck — next kit; a pair whose names share nothing is never learnt); *read right* counts a learnt misread as right (it measures matching).
- **The medical PC's refusal note (P4A, P4C — F-715):** a text the reader refuses writes `<stem>.note`; the watcher posts it to `marg_door` with the header `X-Marg-Note: refused` (one `mi_file` row, capped at 4 KB), retries at every start and census, ends it on *sent / refused 400,413 / taken later / overtaken / expired*; the past is not announced (a sentinel, once); the reason never carries a line of the file. *Overtaken* is judged on text copies only (an Excel export of the same kind does not close it — next kit). Watcher `marg_watch.py` 297cc3d9 since 13:23:48 IST 04-Oct; P4B's 20ec1602 was never placed. The server tells the owner (the reports tile, his line).
- **Two bills in one scan (F-712)** cannot be seen — the asset app keeps one reading per scan; the finding is the parent's.

### 15B.3 · Ordering — the engine on the spine (S470, 04-Oct; D672, D673; F-718, F-719, F-727)

- **Rung 4a of the design is finished.** `order_rules._snapshot_inputs` reads the sale rate (`sales_daily`, returns deducted), the stock (`stock_series` / `closing`), the supplier, the cost, the box, the lot and free (`purchase_lines`) from `spine_read` — the spine's one read door — behind `order.engine_source` (`spine`; `tables` = the old reads, the fallback, also taken on any exception). The item list (names, packings, pack sizes) is still Marg's newest stock list; **no figure is read from it.** Arrivals not yet in Marg by one rule on both bases: a purchase line of the item from that supplier dated on or after the order's own day, matched by key. A sold item never bought is returned under `no_supplier` and counted on the owner's card (39 at the close — purchases before April, the owner's word). Six constants are settings at their present values: `order.pace_days` 28 · `dead_after_days` 60 · `on_order_days` 4 · `in_transit_days` 14 · `lot_history_days` 180 · `engine_source`. Five constants remain inside `plan_line` (next kit). `purchase_app.plan_line(it, cad_days, dead_after=None)` — the one anchored edit.
- **Families.** The spine keys every `sp_*` table on the 20-letter clip of Marg's sale report (F-719, a recorded departure from name + packing); `sp_item` holds 12 keys twice under differing packings (F-727). The engine shows a family as a family and shares its Marg closing equally among the stock-list members (12 orthotic families).
- **The nightly rehearsal (`spine/order_rehearsal.py`) scores the real list.** It reads the kept `order_proposal` rows read-only (the S225 copy of the engine retired — F-718), scores each night's list a week later at 7 and 14 days against the spine's purchases, with stock-outs and money, and writes `/root/finance/spine/orders/order_score_latest.json`; S341's earlier files are in `orders/before_S470/`. One English line a week on the owner's `?old=1` card from the night of 05-Oct. **For the parent:** `spine/orders/` into the state backup.
- **The plan of record (the owner's delegation of 04-Oct: "It's your call on these 4 kits"):** K1 = S470 (done). **K2** the buying model — D672: a review interval per supplier learnt from its bills (Kedar ~3 days, Shivaaz 4, Deepam 7) is the rhythm; the owner's fixed days of 26-Sep stay as an override on his settings page; each medicine's level and usual lot from its own history, scheme included; a rising or new medicine seen in its first week. **K3** Darpan's screen in place of Marg's order entry (the list, *add a medicine*, *not needed*), mock first. **K4** the switch of `order.source` off `marg_sheet` when the bar is met — **D673:** three weeks running, at least 85% of what was bought was on the list beforehand, at least 80% of what was listed was bought within 14 days, no more medicines ran out than under Darpan's sheet.
- **What the relook measured (`S285_ORDERING_RELOOK.md`), kept as the bar's context:** Marg's order sheet is not the whole order (126 entries against 190 bills); the shop's main suppliers deliver every 3–5 days; items are reordered at about 8 days left and about 14 days' worth is bought; 13 of 101 daily sellers touched zero in five weeks (3.6% of item-days); a quarter of the stock by value is over 60 days of sale.

### 15B.4 · The text road — what it reads today, and what it will (the next build)

Text exports from the medical PC are read by title: the item-wise **SALE**, the **STOCK closing** (a stock valuation is taken as nothing), the **ORDER** sheet. Every other text kind is refused and kept (`_captured_txt\refused`) and, since P4C, named to the owner. The Excel road's `signatures.json` knows fifteen report types in nineteen shapes. On 04-Oct the counter exported purchase bill-wise, supplier/item-wise, salt-wise and category-wise as text — all refused, all kept as the samples for the next kit, which gives every report the shop exports a text reader onto the existing loaders (`claude/S285_NEXT_BUILD_PLAN.md`).

F-686, F-687 (Amir's list; the token page) → S452 · F-690, F-691 (the Sarvam count; the unworked queue) → S454 · F-695 (estimate slips) → S454 §3.6 · F-696 (9 of 21) → D666, re-read at S285 · F-701 (compile-only install) open · F-702, F-708 (the phone road) open · F-711 (the cron tick) → P1D · F-712 (two bills) → the parent · F-713 (credit notes as sales) → P3B · F-715 (the packed watcher) → P4C · F-716 (the apply rollbacks) → the parent's S474 · F-717 (the sheet's fallback) next kit · F-718 (the second engine) → S470 · F-719, F-727 (the spine's key) recorded · F-726 (the owner's salt answers) next kit · F-680 corrected.

## 15C · Added at v1.12 — 04 → 05-Oct-2026 (session 295)

*Where §15C and an earlier section disagree, §15C is the later reading and wins. Read from Claude Code's reports S480, S482, S483, S484 and S485, the live pages read on 04 and 05-Oct as the owner (read only), the 05-Oct 01:35 bundle (every file below was changed after it), and the session's five papers. §15B.4 (*the text road … the next build*) is superseded by §15C.1; §15B.3's plan of record is continued by §15C.4.*

### 15C.1 · The text road — every Marg report (S480, 05-Oct 07:25; the medical PC 07:59; D675 a)

- **Marg's Excel export is its text page cut into cells at the column heads.** Measured before the build on three same-moment pairs of 04-Oct — 4,141 cells, none different (`claude/S295_TEXT_READERS_ANALYSIS.md`). So the text road is **one cutter and a spec per report**, in the medical PC's `marg_txt.py`: it turns the text page into the sheet Marg's Excel would have been, and the existing loaders take it from there. The Excel road stays as it was. The plan was that nothing downstream would change; in the event S480 also touched the door's side (`marg_take.py`, `marg_door.py`, `reports_tile.py`, `lib/push_expected.py`, `signatures.json`), and the spine's reader and build had to be mended the same morning (§15C.3) — the proof had covered three reports, not every reader of every sheet.
- **What is read as text now:** purchase (bill-wise, supplier/item-wise, bill/item-wise, supplier-wise), the salt, category and item lists, the short sale statement, the sale return, the stock valuation, the expiry report, the stock register — beside the item-wise sale, the closing stock and the order sheet that were read already. Four kinds carry patient detail (the stock register among them): they are recognised and **never kept at rest** on the server.
- **Where each part lives:** the cutter and the specs only in the medical PC's `marg_txt.py`; the rule that an empty sale sheet is ok / empty in every copy of `marg_report.py` — `/root/finance/` (the copy the door runs), `/root/marg_ingest/` and its `lib/`, manojz's `MargPull\` — the non-finance copies being the same bytes; `signatures.json` the same bytes on the server and manojz.
- **An empty report is an answer (D675 a).** A report whose title, heads and end mark are present with no rows is VERIFIED as EMPTY. For the sale report: *Total No. of Bills: 0* dated before its export day is a no-sale day (`mi_file.reason` *EMPTY — no sale on <date>*); the same blank report made for *today* stays refused, in the staff's words. Empty purchase, return, expiry and list reports are kept, not yet verified — no real empty sample exists.
- **The medical PC's watcher keeps and logs a Marg text whatever Marg named the file** (F-728: `user_<id>.txt` was dropped silently before). Its first start re-offered three days of refused texts — 15 reached the server and were VERIFIED.
- **manojz's daily stock push** (`D:\Downloads\margsync\PUSH_STOCK_DAILY.bat`) runs `push_expected.py` from the **published kit folder** `deploy_kits\S480_MARG_TEXT_READERS\` in the PC clone. That folder is a live dependency and is never pruned.
- **The salts page:** the firm's line and Marg's advertisement line at each page head are never a salt (F-730). An answered section-4 salt still does not reach Amir's sheet (F-726 a, open).
- **Open on this road:** the cutter's column rule for a date-only line (F-734's root — mended at the spine's reader, not at the cutter); one capture second for several texts converted together; a byte-identical re-export is dropped as already taken; *overtaken* judged across Excel and text. All for the next medical-PC kit.

### 15C.2 · The bill chain — what says an export is missing (S482, 05-Oct 09:44; D675 b)

- **`mi_bill_chain`** holds, for each of Marg's two series (the A sale bills, the CN credit notes) and each sale day from 17-Aug onward: the first and last number, the count, the numbers missing before the day (`gap_before`) and inside it (`gap_inside`), and an `empty` mark for a no-sale day. It is rebuilt each time a sale sheet lands (`marg_take.rebuild_chain`, `chain_state`). **A day is complete when its numbers follow the day before; a no-sale day is a day the chain steps over, never a missing export.** The chain now names a missing export by its numbers; the reports tile's banner and the other screens listed below still go by the calendar.
- **A gap is shown, by date and by number, until a person exports those days again** — never presumed a cancelled bill (the owner's rule: *"otherwise every data will be spoiled, the inventory, the sale and everything"*). On Shavez's *Aaj ki reports* page one red line in Hindi; in the owner's reports line in English. Duty `shavez.bill_chain_gap` (DUTY_MAP v6).
- **The first case, standing at this close:** sale bill A003478 and credit note CN00203, between 08-Sep and 09-Sep. The line leaves by itself when the two days' sale reports arrive.
- **Every reader of a sale sheet knows the empty day:** the door, the spine's reader (F-732), the Drive collector (F-733), manojz's outbox — which records `empty_day` and no longer sends it to the finance upload route (F-731; that route answering 422 for an empty day is the parent's to mend).
- **Not yet moved from the calendar to the chain** (`claude/S295_CALENDAR_GLITCH_AUDIT.md`, 24 findings): the reports tile's banner, the spine's closing check, the export watch, Darpan's *kal*, the health legs, the monthly rows after the 7th, the count Sunday; and Amir's flow (our stock figure for every complete day, a waiting card that says since when and for what, refusals kept past midnight). D675 c — *every waiting card says since when and why* — is not built.

### 15C.3 · The spine — its reader, its ties, and what froze it (S483, S484; 05-Oct)

- **What happened:** two sheets made by the new text readers at 07:59 failed the spine's gate from about 08:10 — a bill/item-wise purchase sheet whose date-only lines sat one column off (208 lines read undated, F-734), and the whole-shop category list whose heading carried Marg's own code (F-735). Every ten-minute build ended `GATE FAILED`; `spine.db` stayed at its 08:00 build. S482's builder reported it; S483's fix was stopped by its own walk (a third fault under the first two); S484 went in at 11:35 and the spine built at 11:37:11, gate 13/13, `build_version S484.1`.
- **The reader (`spine/marg_read.py`):** an empty sale day is named by its title; a date-only row is a DATE wherever its one cell sits; a category heading may carry one short code.
- **The build (`spine/spine_build.py`):** purchase-lines exports of one period are ordered by stamp, then SUPPLIER-grouped after BILL-grouped, then more lines after fewer, then md5 — the last of them is the period's authority. **A bill number two suppliers share on one day** (01-Sep, bill 160) is cut, in sheet order, into runs that match each candidate bill, and tied only when exactly one cutting fits (`cut_shared_bill`).
- **F-737, open:** a purchase-lines print made in Marg for one supplier carries no filter line and cannot be told from the whole; exported later than the whole one it would become the period's authority (measured on a scratch store: 208 lines → 5, the gate still passes). A ruling is owed in the chain's consumers kit.
- **The rule this cost (F-734):** a new road for a sheet is proved against every reader of that sheet — the spine's gate among them — before it goes live.

### 15C.4 · Ordering — Darpan's tab, the replay, and the buying model parked (S485, S486; D677, D678)

- **Today the order comes from Darpan's Marg sheet** (`order.source = marg_sheet`, §15B.2) — the owner's own switch of 05-Oct about 17:3x, to stand *"for now"*.
- **S485 is installed and unused (D677).** Darpan's *Kal ka hisaab* has a second tab, *आज का ऑर्डर*, in Devanagari: from `order.darpan_list_time` (09:30) on an order day it lists the day's proposals supplier by supplier; three taps and nothing typed — a medicine held, a medicine added, a quantity − / +; *पक्का* per supplier or *सब पक्का*. On `order.source = darpan` reception's cards show a supplier only after his *Pakka* (`order_proposal.status = darpan_ok`), with exactly his lines; his edits are kept in `order_darpan_edit`. Duty `darpan.order_review` (DUTY_MAP v7). The way back is one tap on the owner's card *Who decides the order* — which sits only on `/finance/porders?old=1` (F-739).
- **Known faults of that tab's first afternoon, all waiting in S486 Part A:** on source `darpan` a sender can still send an unconfirmed supplier from the old page (F-738); reception's heading counts the orthotic card as Darpan's confirmed list, the tab's foot prints a constant 15:00 as a time, the printed sheet's head says *System* (F-739). Darpan has no push subscription — the 09:30 notice reaches nothing.
- **The replay (`claude/S295_ORDER_MODEL_REPLAY.md`):** each method run day by day over the shop's real sales of 04-May … 03-Oct from the real stock, on 124 like-for-like medicines. **The shop as it was: 173 empty-shelf item-days, ₹2.28 lakh average stock, 18.2 supplier orders a week. The engine as it stands (S470's rules): 246, ₹1.59 lakh, 23.8. The refined model: 100, ₹2.03 lakh, 20.2.** What each rule is worth: the shop's own usual lot (100 → 163 without it), orders between reviews (→ 168), the list made with yesterday's sales in it (→ 122), a bounded 7-day rate (→ 118), a largest-day floor (→ 110).
- **K2, the buying model (D678) — briefed, PARKED.** `claude_code_briefs\S486_BUYING_MODEL.md` (774e227f): behind `order.model` = levels | fixed — each supplier reviewed at an interval learnt from its own bills (Kedar on his fixed Monday and Friday); the rate the larger of the 28-day and a bounded 7-day one; quantities in the shop's own lot with the owner's caps as limits; the untouched list made again when yesterday's sale report lands; a largest-day floor; between-review orders only when the stock will not last to the next review; a never-bought medicine shown to Darpan, not dropped; the nightly score judging the engine's own lines; every number a setting on the owner's approvals page. **Not started. Not to be built before a fresh analysis on Fable 5.1 after Saturday 10-Oct night (the owner's word).**
- **K4, the switch,** only after K2 is live and D673's bar is met three weeks running. The owner: *"There is no hurry for shifting for system generated ordering, at the cost of poor logic."*

## 15D · Added at v1.13 — 06 → 07-Oct-2026 (session 296)

*Where §15D and an earlier section disagree, §15D is the later reading and wins. Read from Claude Code's report S488, the two kits the chat built (S490, S491), the live pages read on 06-Oct as the owner (read only), the medical PC as manojz sees it, Drive's two folders, and the 07-Oct 01:35 bundle and 01:40 database — which hold every server file below at the value written here. §15C.1's *open on this road* list and §15C.2's *not yet moved from the calendar* list are brought up to date by §15D.2 and §15D.6.*

### 15D.1 · Dates are compared as dates (S488, 06-Oct 14:18; F-753, F-754)

- **Marg prints a day as dd-mm-yyyy and several tables store it that way** (`stock_snapshot.as_on`, `stock_feed`, `stock_voucher_entered.entered_on`). Sorted or compared as text, 30-09 stands after 04-10. Until S488 the owner's *Stock now* (`stock_app.api_now`), the readiness line, the drift page, Darpan's pipeline leg, `stock_watch.item_info` and the parent's `owner_sheets.consumable_items` each took the newest day that way and read 30-09-2026 all October. **Every sort, MAX and BETWEEN on such a day now goes through a key; the stored values are not rewritten.** The rule for any new statement: make both sides days first.
- **The stock-ahead warning** (*"The stock figure is for … but purchases are known only to …"*) now compares the stock day with the purchase exports' own reach (`MAX(period_to)` in force), not with the calendar. It is silent when the reach covers the stock day. **It cannot tell a day with no purchase from a day not yet exported (F-769, open)** — on such a day it stands, true by the rule.
- **The shelf figure counts filed vouchers.** `shelf_figure.filed_vouchers` reads the append-only entry log by its newest row per batch (counted only while it carries Marg's voucher number), its day as a date; free text in the day falls back to the day of `at`. Before S488 it returned nothing for every item, always (F-754). `boundary_purchases` no longer takes a filed RECEIVE voucher's gain for a purchase dated before the count.
- **The stored flags were re-judged once at install** (`rejudge_s488.py`, behind `finance.db.bak_S488_20261006_141719`): 04-10 66 → 52, 05-10 90 → 77. **What the remaining flags are (F-765, open):** `record_gaps` judges a closing once, with what the spine held at that moment. 37 of the 52 at 04-10, and all 55 first-flagged at 05-10, are explained by sales or purchases that reached the spine afterwards. Until the flag is judged again when a later export lands, the owner's *Marg and shelf moved apart* line over-counts by about that much each day.

### 15D.2 · The medical PC — the reader, the watcher, and what leaves for Drive (S490, S491; F-756, F-764, F-766, F-770)

- **The order sheet's reader takes a packing of any shape** (`marg_txt.py` 7fa5136d, `VERSION` S490). The item master holds 36 packings of 379 that are not N*M (200ML, 30GM, VAIL, 2ML, a bare 1 …). The packing is whatever stands in its place and must start in the same column on every item line of the sheet; a line that does not is refused by its number. No other text reader matches a field by its shape — the rest are cut at Marg's own column heads.
- **What may leave the medical PC for Drive's `FromMedical\refused_text`** (`marg_watch.py` 0a78ae15, S488's file): the body of a refused text only if `_may_leave` admits it — its kind is a purchase, a salt / category / item list, a valuation, an expiry or a stock text, **and** the server's own person patterns (`phi_scan.py`'s, copied letter for letter) find no person word and no mobile-like run outside the shop's Phone line and Marg's footer. Anything else — every sale shape, the sale return, the stock register, the order sheet, an unrecognised text — stays on the medical PC and leaves a `.withheld.txt` and a reason file of two safe lines. It fails closed: an exception, an unknown kind or an oversized text keeps the body on the PC. At each diagnostics pass the watcher sweeps the Drive folder to the same rule. The first sweep (06-Oct 15:40) removed 12 bodies; 20 remain.
- **How a file reaches the medical PC now:** the chat writes it to Drive `ToMedical\_kit` through the file tools, once the owner has approved the folder — each FROM pin read first, the file it replaces kept beside it as `.superseded`, the manifest (CRLF) then the file, both read back. The medical PC's agent installs at its next kit check and restarts the watcher only when `marg_watch.py` itself changed. The owner is not handed a PowerShell line (F-764). A kit's `deliver_*.ps1` stays in the kit as the record of the steps.
- **Two kits for that PC on one day meet at `KIT_MANIFEST.txt`.** The second is delivered on the pins the first left, as a kit of its own (S491 for S488's watcher after S490). **`deploy_kits\S488_DATES_AND_WAITS\deliver_S488.ps1` is superseded and is never to be run.**
- **Open on this road:** a refused text is not offered to a mended reader until the watcher restarts, and a byte-identical re-export is dropped as already taken (F-766) — so, until the next medical-PC kit, a reader mend is delivered together with something that restarts the watcher; the cutter's column rule for a date-only line (F-734's root); one capture second for several texts; the watcher's start line still printing *S480*.

### 15D.3 · Amir's waiting card, and what the owner is told (S488)

- **`stock_app._s446_proof`**, in the state that waits for an export, now says **why** — `no_closing` (Marg's closing after the vouchers has not come), `no_figure` (it has come; our own figure for that day is not made yet — it is made when the day's sale report lands, the empty report on a no-sale day), `pur_behind` (the purchase exports do not reach that day), `no_before` / `rebased` — and **since when** (`closing_day`, `closing_at`, the earliest Marg push after the vouchers).
- **Amir's home card, his steps and the voucher board's line** say it in Roman Hindi, and say when nothing is asked of him (*"Closing stock aa gaya ✓ … Aapko abhi kuch nahi karna."*). Before S488 every state read *"Ab closing stock export kijiye"*, even when the closing had arrived.
- **The owner's lines** say the same in English and name whose turn it is; a proof that has waited `amir.proof_wait_hours` (48) raises a warn line. **A refused report stays on Needs-you for `amir.refused_keep_hours` (36)**, so a refusal late in the evening is still there in the morning, and goes when a VERIFIED file of its type arrives; unrecognised files are one line, not one each.
- **The four new settings** (`amir.proof_wait_hours`, `amir.refused_keep_hours`, `owner.salt_list_days`, `owner.item_lists_days`) are rows in `setting`; they are not yet on the owner's settings card, which is one of S486's parked files.
- **At this close:** the proof stands at *26 of 28*, with Amir's two corrections owed; no voucher was entered after 04-Oct.

### 15D.4 · The owner's own Marg lists are duties (S488; DUTY_MAP v8)

- `manoj.salt_list` (the weekly SALT WISE ITEM LIST, overdue after `owner.salt_list_days` = 8) and `manoj.item_lists` (the monthly CATEGORY WISE ITEM LIST and the item list, `owner.item_lists_days` = 35). **They are the map's first duties with no door** — the work is done in Marg, and no page of ours does it; the owner is told by the orphan-duty lines and by his console. `reports_tile.owner_lists` reads the same limits.
- The map is read from the server's clone, so a new version is live at the pull, before any installer runs. 49 duties at v8; every staff login's list is the same under v7 and v8.

- **`s454_item_name_struck`** is its own table (a strike written as columns on `s454_item_name` would have met that table's key at the next learn). `item_check.strike` / `unstrike` / `struck`; `learn` never learns a struck pair again; after a strike or a put-back the supplier's stored scan checks are cleared so the next compare is fresh.
- On the Items check page (`https://followup.dr-manoj.in/finance/purchase/page/items`) each learnt name has **Wrong**; a collapsed *Struck: N* holds **Put back**. `POST /finance/purchase/api/items/strike` is for the doctor only.
- **D685 (the owner, 06-Oct):** he read all 37 learnt names — *"checked , all look ok"*. None is struck; the three the reports called doubtful stand; the system strikes nothing by itself.

## 15E · Added at v1.14 — 07-Oct-2026 (session 299)

*Where §15E and an earlier section disagree, §15E is the later reading and wins. Read from the brief `S494_AMIR_JOBS_NOTICE_FLAGS.md` (a85cc1f4), Claude Code's report `REPORT_S494.md` (4a9cc5ab), the live pages read on 07-Oct as the owner (read only), the 07-Oct 01:35 bundle and 01:40 database (the code as it stood **before** the kit), manojz's mirror of the medical PC, and the parent's kit folder `S496_POS_STANDIN` on the PC. **The four server files of S494 are the installer's read-backs, not yet a bundle's (the 08-Oct bundle is the first that can hold them).** §15D.1's last bullet (F-765) and §15D.7's *open* marks on F-765 and F-767 are brought up to date by §15E.1, §15E.2 and §15E.8.*

### 15E.1 · A notice is sent by the process that can send it (S494, 07-Oct 12:33; F-767)

- **There are two Pythons on the box, and only one can push.** The web service `clinic-finance` starts with the system Python (`/usr/bin/python3 -m gunicorn`), which has no web-push library. Every cron job runs `/root/wa/venv/bin/python3`, which has. `/root/portal/ring_common.send_push` imports the library inside its own `try`, so from the web service every subscription came back *failed* with no error raised and none logged. Proved on the box before anything was built: the import fails in the first and succeeds in the second.
- **The order sheet's arrival notice was the only push sent from inside a request** (`order_sheet._sheet_notice`, called by `load_file` when the medical PC's sheet lands) — which is why it failed on every phone on 06-Oct while the 17:00 reminder of the same day, sent by the ten-minute job, arrived.
- **Since S494:** `load_file` records the notice on the sheet's own row as pending. `send_pending_notices`, called inside `order_sheet.cron_pass` by the ten-minute tick (`order_rules.py tick`, 05:00–21:50), sends it to the logins of `order.notice_to` — **the same day only, and only within `order.sheet_notice_max_min` (60) minutes of the sheet being taken; two attempts at most.** A notice that cannot be sent inside that window is marked lapsed and is never sent late; so is one whose `order.source` has moved off the Marg sheet. A sheet taken after the day's last tick lapses unsent. The 06-Oct sheet's failed notice was marked lapsed at the first tick.
- **The rule for any new code:** nothing pushes from inside a web request. It writes what is to be said; a job that runs the venv Python says it.
- **Not yet seen on a real sheet** at this writing — the first is Darpan's next order sheet.

### 15E.2 · A stock flag is judged again, and only ever cleared (S494; F-765, F-787)

- `shelf_figure.rejudge` runs at the top of `record_gaps` on every tick. For each first flag of the last `stock.gap_rejudge_days` (7) it asks the spine again whether the movement between the two closings is now explained; if it is, the flag and its carried copies are cleared, with the reason *explained by a report that arrived later (first flagged …; judged again …)*. **It never raises a flag and never flags an unflagged row** — the spine's evidence can shrink for a while (a one-supplier print taken as the whole, F-737), and a verdict must not swing back on that. A second call writes nothing.
- **Its first pass on the live rows (12:40 IST 07-Oct) cleared 178 rows:** flagged items 52 → 15 at the 04-10 closing, 77 → 7 at 05-10, 81 → 10 at 06-10. The owner's line *Marg and the shelf figure moved apart on N items* and Darpan's spot-count roster both read these flags, so both fell with them.
- **What it cannot clear (F-787, open):** a purchase bill keyed into Marg days after the date printed on it. The units arrive in a closing whose window the dated purchase lies outside. Of the 19 first flags left with Marg holding more than explained, 15 match a purchase of exactly those units dated up to seven days before the flag's window. The mend — a purchase placed by the closing in which its units first appear — is the next analysis.

### 15E.3 · Amir's one-time Marg jobs (S494; D686)

- **The table `amir_job`** (in `finance.db`; made by the installer before anything read it): one row per job — a key (`cat:<item>`, `vbill:<vendor>`, `pay:<name>`), whether it is shown, its state, how it was closed, when.
- **The card *Ek baar ke kaam*** sits on his *Marg sudhar* card (`/finance/amir` and every step page; the step is 6). Three kinds of line, each gone when its work is done:
  - **a category line** — *In N item ki category Marg mein ORTHOTIC kijiye*, the item names, and *Marg mein is category ka naam: ORTHOTICS* (the label is read from Marg's own category list, never typed in code). Shown only when he reaches his rename list (stage B). It closes by itself as *seen in Marg* when a category list received after it shows the item under that label; *Kar diya* says it done, and a later list that still shows another category puts it back.
  - **a bill line** — a vendor with no bank details on record, with its newest bill's number and date: *Scan ho gaya* or *Bill nahi mila*. Amir has no scan button — his Scan tile is hidden by the owner's own grants — so reception scans and he taps. It closes by itself when a scan is linked to that vendor's bill or when the bank details are on record.
  - **a tap line** — the Kedar cash-payment entry of ₹310 in Marg: *Kar diya*.
- **The tap** is the form field `job` on the step's own POST, tested before any step's own branch, so it lands from every step and returns to the step it was pressed on. Each tap leaves one audit row. A value for a row not shown, a made-up id or a done row changes nothing. With the table broken or absent every step still renders, and with no open job every step's page is what it was.
- **What the owner is told:** his view of Amir's day ends *one-time Marg jobs: N open*; a job open longer than `amir.job_wait_days` (10) raises one line; a bill Amir says is scanned raises *Bank details to type for <vendor> — its bill is scanned*; *Bill nahi mila* raises a *could not find* line; an item absent from a category list received after his tap raises a line naming it.
- **The duty** `amir.marg_jobs` (DUTY_MAP v9; door: his card; allowed 10 days; its `due_sql` answers *nothing to do* on an empty table).
- **Seeded on 07-Oct:** bill lines for AGARWAL SURGICALS AND MEDICALS (closed at once — its bill of 07-Sep is linked to a scan), RAMA MEDICOSE and KUSHAGRA MEDICAL AGENCY; the Kedar line; four category rows (BRACE TYLOR UNISON, SKIN TRACTION HOPE, TENNIS ELBOW L HOPE, TENNIS ELBOW M HOPE), hidden until stage B.
- **The four category rows were overtaken the same afternoon:** the owner corrected the four in Marg's item master himself and exported the category list (captured 13:47:35 IST, accepted, archived VERIFIED). In that list all four stand under ORTHOTICS (85 items there; COMMON 29, NEW SEPT 2025 10, OTHERS 220), so the rows close as *seen in Marg* without ever reaching Amir's card.
- **Open wording (F-786):** `_s446_left` says *waiting for the closing-stock export* for every proof state that is not *proved*, including a proof that has come back wrong.

### 15E.4 · The owner's Needs-you list — one fact, one line (S494; F-771)

A missed spot count printed twice — once from `needs_you_lines`' own block and once from the notices path. It is one line now, keyed `spot_missed`, the newest of the week. Two notices of another kind still make two lines.

### 15E.5 · Kedar's July balance, and whose hand a money figure is (D686; F-785)

- The server's July bills for KEDAR PHARMACEUTICAL come to ₹98,240; the bank's NEFT was ₹97,930; the difference is the old ₹310. The owner's ruling (24-Sep): *"do an adjustment entry of cash payment and close this old balance in books."* On 07-Oct he typed **Paid 98240** on July's vendor sheet himself (`/finance/purchase/page/pay/2026-07`; the sheet has no note box — the split is recorded in the Archive and in D686). Carried in is blank, the payable ₹98,240, the month still a draft. The matching ₹310 cash-payment entry in Marg is Amir's tap line (§15E.3).
- **A figure that moves money or a payable on a live page is typed by the owner, or by a staff member on their own screen.** The assistant shows the row, the box and the figure, and reads the page back afterwards. It does not save one by any route.

### 15E.6 · When the bank's statement has not come (the parent's S496, 07-Oct about 13:45; the owner's design of 12:17)

- **What the pharmacy already did:** with no bank row for a day, `darpan_kal.compute_day` takes the day's online figure from Marg's own non-cash entries, marks it provisional, and decides the day again when the bank's file lands. The pharmacy never waited for the bank; the clinic's match did.
- **The owner's design:** *"when Darpan post kal ka hisab, then if the bank has not populated the report then he gets the option to write the total figure of yesterday's UPI from reading it from the POS machine … And that is subject to my confirmation … they open no new screens."* Built by the parent's chat for the pharmacy and the clinic together, with this chat's agreement to its editing three of this project's files: `darpan_kal.py`, `darpan_kal.html`, `finance_ui/finance_approvals.html` (and its own `bank_standin.py`, `bank_mpr_status.py`, `clinic_money.py`).
- **What changed on this project's side:** on *Kal ka hisaab*, only while the bank's statement for the day is missing, a box for the POS total at the moment of posting; `compute_day` answers three more keys — `online_source` (`bank` | `pos` | `marg`), `marg_online_p`, `standin`; a typed total waiting for the owner stands first on his approvals page (kind `pos_typed`) and counts only after he confirms it; the bank's own file, when it lands, replaces it. **The owner's line *Darpan: N items where his word and the data differ* now counts a waiting POS total — its wording is this project's to change.**
- **S486's pins for those three files are stale from that install**; the Register's §S299 carries the new ones at the kit's own hashes.

### 15E.7 · Amir works from *Amir ka kaam* alone (D690)

The parent's *Aaj ka kaam* lists (S493) have a list for Amir; the owner keeps it switched off — *"so that he is not confused and adopts to the current flow completely because that has the relevant checks built into it already."* Nothing is added to that list for him, and a new duty of his needs no line there. His jobs, his day and his proofs are on *Amir ka kaam* and its step pages. If the owner switches the list on one day, its lines for Amir are written from this project.

### 15E.8 · The medical PC — what the morning of 07-Oct showed

- **A long export can be captured cut short.** A bill-wise sale statement for 06-Sep → 06-Oct was captured at 05:30 at 106,218 bytes, stopping at its page 86 with no end line; no second capture followed. The reader refused it, rightly, and its body stayed on the medical PC (a sale shape never leaves — §15D.2). `marg_watch`'s settle time is 60 ms; whether Marg was still writing or the export itself stopped is not known. **For the next medical-PC kit:** a text is taken only when it has stopped growing for a longer settle and carries its end line, or is held and offered again.
- **The one-day statements of 08-Sep and 09-Sep were taken and verified** the same morning, so the bill chain has no gap and the gap line is gone from Shavez's tile and from Needs-you.
- **Shavez's tile files a refused range report under the day its range ends on (F-788, open)** — it read as *06-Oct refused* until that day's own report arrived.
- **How such a file is read (F-784):** by its shape — line counts, page markers, the end line, the headings, a hash — or with the name and number columns cut out by the command itself. Never by its first lines.

### 15E.9 · What is still to build — what moved since §15D.6

Done since: F-765, F-767, F-771 (S494); the owner's three notes of 24-Sep. In order now — (1) S486, parked until the Fable re-read after 10-Oct night; (2) bills keyed later than their date (F-787) — analysis first; (3) Amir's flow made calendar-safe, with F-786; (4) the chain in place of the calendar, with F-769, F-737's ruling and F-788; (5) the next medical-PC kit (F-766; a long export captured whole; F-734's root); (6) small — an answered salt on Amir's sheet (F-726 a), the settings on his card, F-777, the `pos_typed` wording, the parent's two flooring asks; (7) the remaining screens onto the spine; (8) count #2; (9) near-expiry and the returns desk's phase 2; (10) scanned bills into Marg without waiting for Amir; (11) the old duplicates retired and the pharmacy as its own program. `claude/S295_PENDING_BUILDS_05OCT.md` is still the list of record for the rungs.

### 15E.10 · Faults of this period, the one-line map

F-765 (the shelf-gap flag judged once) → S494 · F-767 (the arrival notice failed on every send) → S494, not yet seen on a real sheet · F-771 (the spot-count line twice) → S494 · F-777 (a duty that can never fall to zero; the parent's finding on this project's map) → open · F-784 (a patients' file read by its first lines; the assistant's) → read by shape · F-785 (a money entry promised as the chat's step; the assistant's) → the owner's hand · F-786 (*waiting for the export* said of a wrong proof) → open · F-787 (a bill keyed later than its date raises a flag) → open, the next analysis · F-788 (a refused range report filed under a day) → open · F-789 (four blockers in the S494 brief's two drafts; the assistant's) → caught by two independent reads · F-766, F-769, F-737 open as before.

## 15F · Added at v1.15 — 09 → 11-Oct-2026 (session 303)

### 15F.1 · The build charter (D705) — how this system is built from now

Every Sanjeevni build and correction follows `claude/S303_BUILD_CHARTER.md`; the builder reads `claude_code_briefs/SANJEEVNI_BUILD_CHARTER.md` first.

**The ten rules:**
1. Marg's facts come from the spine (§4A); people's entries are written once; conclusions are computed, never kept as truth.
2. One fact, one place.
3. **Two stock figures, never mixed in one judgment:** the *Marg figure* (Marg's closing carried by the spine's movements) and the *shelf figure* (the last physical count carried the same way).
4. A stored conclusion follows its inputs.
5. A correction is a new row; a closed count is sealed.
6. One door per action.
7. One screen, one person, one job.
8. One wordbook (Roman Hindi for staff, English for the owner, quantities through `qty_words`).
9. Retire what you replace.
10. Repairs through the system's doors, never DELETE.

**The app's sections are this Book's:** capture · spine · purchase · sale and money · stock · ordering · returns · watch.

**The order of work:** S486 re-read → **rung 4b (stock on the spine — one module for both figures; loss lines, flags and traces computed; the three count tables through one view; `push_expected`, `marg_shadow`, `expected_units` retired after a side-by-side week)** → late bills (S498) → 4c → 4d, 4e, 4f → rung 5, P4/P5.

The charter sits beside the parent's `BUILD_NORM_v1` (D704).

### 15F.2 · The morning reports, judged (S500, live 22:56 IST 10-Oct; D694)

`/finance/reports/aaj` (*Aaj ki reports*) carries the steps of both reports with Marg's own screens (`/finance/reports/kaise/sale`, `/kaise/stock`). For each report:
- it says *Sahi hai* or *Galat hai · koshish 1 / 2*, with the step to redo;
- a second wrong try reads *Doosri baar bhi galat*;
- the owner is told by ntfy only then, or when a report is not right by 10:00 (`reports_watch.py`, table `marg_report_alert`, settings `reports.tries` 2, `reports.late_hhmm` 10:00, `reports.closing_min_share` 90, `reports.try_gap_min` 3, `reports.alert` on).

**A closing is short when** it has fewer items than 90 % of the median of the last 7 closings: it is marked wrong and refused at `/finance/stock/api/snapshot` (409, F-799).

Amir's voucher proof reads beyond the first closing (F-801; refined in §15F.4). Amir's salt words say text or Excel (F-805).

### 15F.3 · Darpan's Stock milaan — today's work (S502, live 23:20 IST 10-Oct; D699)

`/finance/stockmatch`, in order, shows:
- *Aaj ki ginti* (the spot list), each answered row with **Galat likha? Badlo** the same day: `POST /api/spot/fix` → `stock_watch.correct_roster`. The correction is judged on the replaced count's own expected figure; the old point is `superseded`, never deleted;
- the Sunday plan when asked;
- open swap, reason and *Bina bill?* questions only while unanswered;
- *Stock batao*;
- *Kuchh gadbad hai?*;
- *Pichhle 7 din mein kam nikla* — unvoided lines of the last 7 days, in plain words.

**Gone from his page:** the 06-Sep tables, the answered pairs, *Orthotics band*, *Dobara ginna hai* (its route refuses a non-owner — F-813), the round heading, *System ne dekha*. The owner's Loss desk keeps them.

**The TYRO BR line of 08-Oct is voided** (`stock_watch_loss_line.void_at`, a stopgap until 4b computes loss lines); TYRO BR heads the next morning list. **TRUECEPROL OD of 10-Oct reads 13:7**; the 12:01 row is off the 06-Sep count.

### 15F.4 · Amir's vouchers — one judgment on his card and his board (S520 23:45 IST 10-Oct; S521 05:07 IST 11-Oct)

**`_s446_proof`:** an item is proven when the FIRST closing after the vouchers (only when on the before-closing's basis) or the NEWEST shows it moved by exactly its vouchers.
- The first proves a voucher keyed right — a later sale cannot unprove it. The four LS belt sizes share one 20-letter name in the sale report, so the PC's computed figure cannot place their sales (F-830).
- The newest proves a correction Amir made in Marg after a wrong check.
- A middle closing alone never passes an item.

**Amir's board** (`/finance/stock/page/amir`) takes its section-2 line, its chip and its renames gate from `amir_stage` — the same check as his card — for every login. `_s444_renames` (his step-6 rename line) does the same (F-838).

**At 05:2x IST 11-Oct:** *Orthotic: sab sahi ✓ · Naam badlo: 22 naam*; the board lists the 23 renames (1 seen in Marg); no medicine voucher shows for him until the renames are proven (stage B → C, D649).

## 15G · Added at v1.16 — 11-Oct-2026 (session 307)

### 15G.1 · The charter v1.1 (D707) — fourteen rules

§15F.1's ten stand. Six are widened and four are new; the builder reads `claude_code_briefs/SANJEEVNI_BUILD_CHARTER.md` v1.1 (f64e65fa), the owner's paper is `claude/S307_BUILD_CHARTER_v1_1.md`.

- **1b · The source's reach and health.** A judgment that reads the spine states the reach it needs (`sp_export`'s `date_to` for sales and purchases up to the day it judges) and answers *not yet* when the spine is short — never *wrong*. The spine's health is a loud line for the owner, red within an hour of its first failed build (the parent's S525 leg since 08:0x 11-Oct). A kit that makes a page depend on the spine or a scheduled job reads that job's state at install and refuses to place while it is red.
- **2 · The test for one fact, one place.** The brief lists by grep every place that answers the question the build touches; the kit leaves one.
- **4 · An event is a record, never a gate.** The gate recomputes from the inputs on every read; a stored conclusion carries the stamp of its inputs; when the inputs move and it cannot be recomputed at once, the owner's line says it is stale.
- **7 · The install-day state.** A brief that changes a staff screen reads the live data as it will stand on install day, names that state, and writes the words for it; a state no screen has words for is a blocker.
- **8 · Never a figure to type; words built once.** A line names the voucher or the bill to check, with the date of the figure it shows; the words live in the row the screen reads, never in a second file.
- **9 · Dead code is a defect**, removed by the build that finds it.
- **11 · Time.** One clock (the server's, IST); ISO stored; dd-mm-yyyy only on a screen; never the two compared. A closing is placed by its capture moment (`push_expected._gap_side`: before 09:00 = the end of the previous day; 22:00 or later = the end of its day; between = unusable for a judgment), never by its printed day alone. Days a staff member owes are counted in his visit days.
- **12 · Identity.** An item is the spine's key (`sp_item.k20` with the spine's aliases and families), never a bare name or a 20-letter clip; items sharing a key are judged on their net. A rename is read back from Marg's own export by that identity, tolerant of spacing and case, with one owner door to accept a sighting.
- **13 · The door and the line follow the screen.** What a screen hides from a person, that screen's door refuses for that person; the owner's line for a staff duty counts the set the staff screen shows.
- **14 · A staff page is light** — under two seconds and 100 KB; the owner's data on the owner's routes.

`BUILD_NORM` v2 (§3, §5, §6 widened; §7 the brief; §8 the health of a dependency) is proposed to the parent, whose file it is.

### 15G.2 · Amir's page — the rebuild in three kits (D706) and Kit 1 (S524, live 07:59 IST 11-Oct)

**The audit** (`claude/S307_AMIR_PAGE_AUDIT.md`) found four roots behind the errors of the last weeks: *did his voucher reach Marg?* answered in five places by three rules; the proof's "our figure" the PC's computation, blind to vouchers; verdicts stored once; and the page itself judging one fact by two rules under four generations of old code. The decision: one rebuild in three kits — one judgment (S524), the page (S528), renames by identity (folded into S528 at this close).

**S524 — one judgment, from the spine.**
- `stock_app._s446_proof` reads the seven orthotic batches by key: `sp_close` and `sp_move` within the reach `sp_export` gives (at the install: `last_sale` 09-10, `last_pur` 08-10). The tries are the first clean closing after the vouchers' basis closing R and the newest the reach covers; a key proves at any try when Marg moved by exactly its vouchers net of the spine's own moves; a wrong row names the voucher to check and never a figure.
- **The stage is recomputed on every read** (`amir_stage`); the stored `stock_stage_event` is a record, with a once-only `recheck` row when the verdict changes. Stage A stays A while an orthotic batch exists or an orthotic line is owed a voucher; the card says so (*ek orthotic line ka naya voucher Doctor sahab banayenge*).
- The board's line, the hub's step 7, the orthotic section (`stockmatch.section_state` c3) and Needs-you all read the one judgment. `api_pad_vouchers_entered` refuses an unreleased batch to a non-checker (403 `not_released`). DUTY_MAP v10's `amir.count_vouchers` counts released batches only. `shelf_figure.filed_vouchers` places a voucher by the closing's capture window. `marg_take._rename_verify` refuses a short closing as a sighting.
- **Retired:** `_proof_state`, `_proof_pur_to`, `_proof_basis`, the `_proof_state` blocks of `_s436_board_extra`, stockmatch's old c3, `_s444_renames`' report read, the `should` figure, the `rebased` reason, the unreleased-batch count.

**Part 0 — the spine's three silent days (F-850).** From 08-Oct 09:10 to 11-Oct 07:59 every ten-minute build failed its gate 11 of 13 and kept the old spine: Marg's item-wise purchase report prints the last 8 characters of a long bill number glued to the item name (`A000207_TYRO BR`, `06202627ANKLE BINDER BAMBOO M`, the GUNINA `L2678591`), and `marg_read.py` refused such rows while `spine_build.py` could not tie the ones it read. Now: a 6–8 character piece, an optional `_` or space, then the item is ITEM_GLUED; `tie_piece()` finds the one bill of that supplier in the export's period whose number contains the piece and the line carries the whole number. The two 08-Oct readings were re-read in place (46 → 51 lines). Gate 13 of 13; the live spine swapped 07:59:34 and the cron rebuilt by itself at 08:00:45. **Until this kit nothing in the pharmacy's own screens said the spine was behind** — rule 1b and the parent's leg (S525) answer that.

**What the first live read taught (F-851).** The card named TYNOR WRIST SPLINT RT M ELAST *wrong* on the closings of 05-10 and 07-10 while its second unit had been keyed on 08-10 — outside the spine's purchase reach. The brief's rule allowed the newest closing in reach as a try; the charter's intent (1b) was not carried into the words. Kit 2: *wrong* only when the newest clean closing lies inside both reaches.

### 15G.3 · The owner's rulings of 11-Oct (D708, D709)

**D708 — a staff screen carries only "do this" lines.** At 09:4x Amir, told by his card to *check voucher 6* and offered *Voucher kholiye*, opened his board and took back a right voucher with its own *Wapas le liya* button. The owner: *"these commands are confusing even for me … the system should be such that these options should not be available to our staff of this IQ and this level … I am stuck up with this work."* From now: no take-back button, no *check voucher*, no *fix and export again*, no judgment sentence on any staff screen; every verdict about a voucher, a rename or a figure goes to the owner's hub and Needs-you, and he decides what a staff member is told; a line a staff member cannot act on does not appear; the clear of a recorded voucher is the checker's door only.

**D709 — Amir's spellings are the names.** Twice that morning the chat asked for exact spellings; the owner: *"now it has become a never-ending task."* A rename is verified by identity — the first 20 letters distinct, spacing and case ignored — on the first closing captured after the tick, whatever day it is printed for. The three shoulder immobilisers he renamed exactly at the owner's word stand; the other eight spellings (UNIS / UNISO / ELASTI / XXXL) are accepted.

### 15G.4 · What Amir's board still does today, and what Kit 2 (S528) retires

- **The take-back** (`stock_amir.html` ~180) for every login → the checker only (F-852).
- **Server ko bhejo** (section 2, ~147) posts to `/api/pad/marg_answer/<count>` — an attachment for the owner, never the stock pipeline; a closing exported from Amir's own Marg login (*Operator : AMIR*) is refused as unrecognised (F-853) → the box leaves the staff board; the hub says what the server last received.
- **`item_alias.verify_closing`** skips an export printed as on a day before the tick's day and one captured before the tick, and matches exactly or on a 29-character clip — so a day's ticks cannot verify on that day's closing printed as on the previous day (F-854) → the capture moment and identity (D709).
- **The payload** 475 KB per open; the S444 branches; the *Excel* words; *Purani list*; the salt line said twice (F-839) → rules 8, 9, 14.
- Bills' *since when* → `purchase_bill.first_seen_day`; owed days in visit days (rule 11); DUTY_MAP v11.
