## 15H · Added at v1.17 — 11-Oct-2026, the evening (session 308)

### 15H.1 · Kit 2 of Amir's page — S528_AMIR_PAGE, live 16:25 IST 11-Oct (D706, D708, D709)

**What it is.** The judgment reaching Amir's screens rightly, and the renames by identity. The chat cut D706's plan at its brief: Kit 2 = stock_app, item_alias, Amir's board page and four anchored places in amir_day (so that no "check / open / take back" sentence stays live); Kit 3 = the page rebuilt whole, scoped in `claude_code_briefs/S528_KIT3_DEFERRED.md`. The brief was written from two design maps of the live bytes (the S524 TOs rebuilt offline from the 11-Oct bundle through the kits' own patchers) and held to two independent reads and a second-round read — eight blockers caught before the paste line (F-865).

**The proof's verdict (§15G.2's open rule, F-851 repaired).** A key is *wrong* only when the try at the newest clean closing, inside both reaches (sales and purchases to the day it judges), gives False; a False at an earlier try while the newest closing lies outside reach is `pending_reach` → *waiting*, the reason the newest closing's. **The proof's window is a batch's FIRST non-empty entry** (`_s446_window`): a take-back by the checker and a fresh entry by Amir never move the window past a closing that already carries the voucher. On the live data that is what proved the seven orthotic keys on the 09-10 closing the same afternoon.

**The door (F-852, D708).** `clear` is the checker's only; a non-checker's POST on an already-entered key is refused (*Yeh voucher likha ja chuka hai*); the replies are Hindi for every role; `entered_on` is stored ISO for new rows and rendered through one formatter. The rename tick's clear is checker-only; the upload box (`api_pad_marg_answer`) is checker-only and gone from the staff board (F-853).

**One source of the stage words** — `_s521_stage_words(con, st)`: `lines[{hi, anchor}]` composed by one rule (the stage's line, then the B line while `renames_visible`), `card_hi`, `owner_en`, `owner_lines`. The staff line carries no verdict and no promise that is not true; the owner's lines name the voucher by its one number, the rename questions, the amber and both-printed names, a Marg number typed on two keys. Amir's card, step 7's extra line, the owner's stage line and `_s446_left` read it. **Retired:** `_amir_board`, `_s436_board_extra`, `_s452_board_roman` + `S452_REASONS_ROMAN`, `_s446_board_filter`, `_s521_stage_proof`, `_s521_wrong_hi`, `_s521_export_hi`, `S521_EXPORT_HI`, stock_app's `S488_TAIL_HI` / `_s488_tail_hi`, amir_day's `_s446_wrong_lines` and the card's stage branch tree, the stage's renumbering, the take-back and the second entry on a staff screen, the *"agle stock export mein dikhna chahiye"* promise, `api_snapshot`'s rename call, the day rule and the exact-string match of `verify_closing`. **Dead, left for Kit 3:** amir_day's `_s488_wait_html`, `_s488_tail_hi`, `S488_TAIL_HI`, `_s488_proof`, `_s488_why`.

**One light board** — `_amir_board_light`: 5.7 KB as amir (was 475 KB), 37.7 KB as the owner; one `amir_stage` per request; `_voucher_seq` the one numbering for the card, both boards, the statement and the xlsx; `stock_amir.html` replaced whole (no *Wapas* for a non-checker, no upload box, the questions block with Accept for the checker, the *Aakhri closing stock jo server ko mila* line).

**Renames by identity (F-854, D709)** — `item_alias.verify_closing`: `captured` required; a row is judged by a closing captured after its tick whatever day it is printed for; seen on the full fold (upper, spaces collapsed, trailing dots stripped) or its 29-clip, the old name gone; a near-miss — the alnum fold, or a printed spelling that is a prefix of the new name or carries it as a prefix (the shorter ≥ 20 letters) — or both names printed, or a collision with a third item, is a `question` for the owner (his Accept on the board; Amir never sees it); a rename made in Marg without a tick is read back; `untick` writes `undone_at`; every event in `marg_item_rename_event`; one `sight_closing` applies the short-closing gate for every caller. **At install, the 22 ticks judged from the 10:35 closing in the collector's archive: 15 verified, 6 questions, 2 not seen** — Marg clips the list's long names at its field width (F-861); the owner accepted the six at 17:00; the two spellings are taken into the list in Kit 3.

### 15H.2 · The owner's ruling of 11-Oct, 17:06 (D712) — the bill line comes from the scan

*"Maal aa gaya, Marg mein bill baaki: N"* (`stock_watch.bill_pending_lines`: an arrival tapped by reception + no purchase in the spine for that item, supplier and date) is true and yet misleading — a wrong tap or a bill keyed under another name makes a line Amir can do nothing about (F-864). From Kit 3: *"Scan hua bill Marg mein baaki: N — kholiye"* — the scanned supplier bills Marg's purchase export does not yet carry (the rule of the owner's own *scanned and not yet in Marg* line), opening the picture so he keys the bill from it; the arrival tap stays reception's own record and never reaches Amir; an arrival with nothing scanned after a few days is one owner line in Needs-you.

Part B adds second copies of the lot, the supplier interval and days of cover, and a Marg-vs-shelf judgment beside `shelf_figure.record_gaps`; the rhythm reads `purchase_bill`, not the spine; B.6's *गिन लें* and B.5's chips are against D708. **Verdict:** Part A (F-738, F-739, F-717, the fold) as its own kit with four changes; **Part B re-briefed on the one stock function rung 4b builds** — Kit 3 → S486 A → rung 4b → S486 B. Its PLANNED lines lapse 18-Oct and are not renewed.

## 15I · Added at v1.18 — 11 → 13-Oct-2026 (session 310)

*Read from Claude Code's reports S533, S536, S537 and S539, the four briefs and their six reads, the chat's maps (`A_anchors.md`, `B_anchors.md`, `MAP_S537.md`), the live pages read 12-Oct, Darpan's purchase-order export of 12-Oct 14:35, and the 13-Oct 01:35 bundle. Where this section and an earlier one disagree, this one is the later reading and wins.*

### 15I.1 · Amir's page, as it is now (S533, S536)

- **One reading of the facts per request.** `amir_day.state()` reads once; every step's row is built from it; the Hindi words and the owner's English come from the same row, so the card, step 7 and the owner's Needs-you cannot disagree. Writes made while a page is read go through `settle()` — append-only and audited.
- **Append-only records:** `amir_step_log(id, day, step, done_at, by_user, note, replaces)` and `amir_bill_answer(id, bill_id, day, reason, by_user, at, note, replaces)`; readers take the newest row per key. The old tables are read-only history (their rows copied once, closed days `note='close'`).
- **A bill's since-when** is `purchase_bill.first_seen_day`, set on insert.
- **The bill line rests on the scan (D712):** *"Scan hua bill Marg mein baaki: N — kholiye"* — a supplier bill reception scanned that Marg's purchase export does not yet carry, opening the picture. The reception arrival tap is reception's record and appears on neither Amir's card nor his board; an arrival with nothing scanned after some days is the owner's Needs-you line.
- **The renames are finished:** 23 of 23 verified (the last two by taking Amir's spellings as the names, D709).
- **"Live orthotic ordering can start"** appears on the owner's Needs-you only once the count is at stage C or done, dated from the first stage-C release, for two days.
- **The duty map** (v12) holds amir_day's three SQL strings as shared text (never views); the walk asserts they are equal.

### 15I.2 · Vouchers: the void, the settle, and the guard (S537, S539 — D715)

- **A voucher can be voided** (`stock_voucher_void`, append-only: count, round, kind, batch, by whom, why). Every reader honours it — the board, the card, the statement, the loss piles, the shelf figure, the duty map (v12) — through one `_void_sql`.
- **An item can be settled for a count** (`stock_voucher_settle`): `_voucher_pending` never raises it again for that count.
- **The guard:** a voucher line — new, or already made and waiting — is held from Amir when Marg's newest closing for that item (by its **full printed name**, so sizes stay apart), less the vouchers made and not yet keyed, would go below zero. A held line is the owner's, on Needs-you; the release of the next lot skips a held batch. A renamed item prints its new name in Marg's closing and reads *not readable* to the guard — offered, counted in the owner's summary (F-874, until rung 4b). "Never past the shelf figure" waits for rung 4b.
- **A tied arrival is never "bill missing"**: `stock_watch.bill_pending_lines` skips an arrival whose `purchase_order_line.billed_bill_no` is set.
- **Why it was built (F-872):** voucher 29 asked Amir to issue −1 ANKLE BINDER BAMBOO L that Marg had shown at 0 since 04-10; the "still owed" arithmetic counted from 6-Sep alone.
- **The owner's settlements so far for count #1:** ANKLE BINDER BAMBOO L (voucher 29 void; Darpan's spot count asked — ANKLE BINDER L BAMBOO in Marg now); **NORTIMER TAB** (voucher 11 void — strips with Darpan, being sold; surplus later by a fresh count and a new voucher). Voucher 11's other five lines are **STOCK ISSUE voucher 30** (NIMREX P −11 · NUROKIND LC −5 · NURVION LC TAB −38 · OLMETIME H 20 −45 · ONDEM MD 4 −35).

- **Where it lives:** the reception's ordering screen `/finance/porders/s454/order` (`porders_s454.py`, a card per supplier) and its printed sheet `/finance/porders/s454/sheet.pdf` (`order_sheet_pdf.build`, the *Order sheet print karo* button). Marg's own *PENDING ORDERS (PURCHASE)* export reaches the server through the collector (captured on manojz under `MargArchive\ORDER_PENDING\`).

- **Where it lives today:** `order_sheet.py` (012e21c9) keeps each line of Darpan's export (`order_sheet_line`, keyed by entry number and printed name) with the system's own state — `to_order`, `in_line`, `ordered`, `old`, `lapsed`, `closed_marg`. The screen (`porders_s454.py` 9c463e7c) and the printed sheet (`order_sheet_pdf.py` 6e7a5e1a) chose what to show from those states and from day counts (`order.old_done_days` 7, `order.old_show_days` 60), from `purchase_order.status` and the bill-scan tie, and from a match against Marg's purchases.
- **What that did (12-Oct export, 26 lines, 12 suppliers):** COCYP (OP-0334) hidden — its order tied to a bill scan while Marg still printed it; nine lines Marg had closed still shown under *Purane pending* (Ravi's three "arrived 05-10" among them); CHYMORAL's order of 02-10 on *Maal aaya?* after Marg dropped it; the heading counting only lines dated that day (22); no units (F-876 … F-878).
- **The design (S543 v3, `claude_code_briefs/S543_ORDER_SHEET_MARG.md` b6f38033):**
  - **One reading, `open_order()`:** the lines of the newest export, ranked by its own capture stamp (a late-loaded older export never becomes Marg's word, and never pulls a line off); every line in exactly one group — *to order* · *in the WhatsApp line* · *ordered* · *old*; a line Marg no longer prints is on no ordering page.
  - **Ordered** = ordered and either still under *Maal aaya?* or made fewer than `order.sheet_max_age_days` (7) days ago; after that, if Marg still carries it, *old* — with its order day and, when its goods came, *"maal aa gaya · Marg mein baaki"* — and **never** *Dobara order karo* (UPRISE-type old lines keep the button).
  - Marg's RECEIVE and PENDING kept on each line (healed from the export file by the code itself); a part-received line reads *"Marg: x aaya, y baaki"*.
  - **Units** through `qty_words` (patte, botal, vial, tube, nag; English on the owner's view); a whole-unit item reads packs.
  - **The heading:** *"Marg mein khula order (12-10 14:35) · 12 supplier, 26 dawa"* — the same figure on the printed sheet's head and on Darpan's card; Darpan's *"the order he made that day"* (22) is a different question with its own words.
  - **Marg's own order printed** (`marg_order_pdf.py`, landscape, Marg's eleven columns and TOTAL, the export's time at the foot) beside *Order sheet print karo*; **both buttons shown while Marg's open order has a line**.
  - *Maal aaya?* and the arrival door leave out / refuse an order Marg has closed (judged by the order's own entry numbers, `ext_ref`).
  - DUTY_MAP v13: reception's *Order karna hai* and *Maal aaya?* counts read the same open order (the arrival count's tables `main.`-qualified so the staff's floored *Aaj ka kaam* reads 5, not 0).
- **Three reads:** R1 4 blockers, R2 2, R3 1 — all taken in (`03_WORKING_PAPERS\S312\`).

### 15J.2 · The medical PC's sale reader — S546 (F-892; F-893 open)

- `marg_txt.py` S546 (a59a222f) on the medical PC since 10:19 IST 13-Oct (Drive `ToMedical\_kit`, placed 10:03:24; backups `marg_txt_S490.py.superseded`, `KIT_MANIFEST_S499.txt.superseded`).

So a reader repair reaches a refused text by a fresh export (as on 13-Oct, 10:40) or the next morning's start. The reports page tells staff *"dobara banaiye"* for every refusal, the reader's included.

### 15K.5 · The stock proof of count #1, stage C — judged (S569, S570, S572; F-905, F-906)

- **Amir entered all 30 stage-C medicine vouchers on 15-Oct (09:14–10:00).**
- **The reach rule, as it is now** (`stock_app.py` 050587df): `_s524_tryable` tries a closing when the sale and purchase exports cover it; an export covers a closing when captured after it **or** when, by `_s524_end_day` (a capture before 09:00 stands for the end of the previous day; a same-day capture inside shop hours stands for no day's end), it stands for a day at or after the closing's day. So the morning order — the sale report ≈05:10, the closing ≈05:17 — is judged the same morning; a purchase report exported before its day ended is still, rightly, not enough.
- **The owner's waiting line** names the closing the proof uses (*"Marg's closing of 15-10 arrived 16-10 05:17"*) and what it waits for (*"a purchase export that reaches that day (Amir)"*). Amir's card says only *"· Purchase report nikaaliye"* while it waits.

- **Who changes what:** numbers, emails, picks, *Add a supplier*, the merge and the switch — **the owner only** (staff hand him a new number on paper). Bank details — as before (the doctor and Shavez; *Verify* the doctor). Every change is listed at the foot of the page (who, when, …old4 → …new4).

## 15M · Added at v1.22 — 18-Oct-2026 (session 324)

### 15M.1 · Stock on the spine — rung 4b (S577, live 12:14 IST 18-Oct)

- **`/root/finance/stock_figure.py` is the one stock function (charter 1–3, 11, 12).** For an item it gives two figures, never mixed, each on the closing it stands on:
  - **Marg's figure:** Marg's closing by the item's printed name, from the newest usable capture, plus the spine's movements after that closing's end day.
  - **The shelf figure:** the newest count (the 6-Sep count, a spot point, or a desk correction, through one view of the three count tables) carried forward the same way, plus arrivals not yet in Marg.
  - **An item with no count has no shelf figure.** It never borrows Marg's.
- **The capture rule is unchanged (charter 11).** A closing captured before 09:00 stands for the end of the previous day; one captured at or after 22:00 stands for the end of its own day; one printed for an earlier day stands for that day; a same-day capture inside shop hours stands for none.
  - **For any end day, the newest usable capture is used.**
  - **A closing's moment is the export's own stamp,** never the PC's 22:30 re-send (F-928).
- **One identity** (`key_of`, `printed_forms`): the spine's key by the printed name, then the ticked renames (`item_alias.done_rows`, with the accepted printed spellings), then the clip.
  - A renamed item is read under both names.
  - Sizes that share Marg's 20-letter name are told apart at a closing (`sp_close_name`) and are one family between closings — **approximate**, never judged per size.
- **The spine's new table:** `sp_close_name` holds every accepted full closing capture by printed name (22,315 rows, 55 captures at install). `sp_close` and the gate are unchanged (13/13).
- **The voucher check (D715, both halves):**
  - below zero on Marg's newest usable closing (renamed items judged, not "not readable");
  - **past the shelf figure** — an ISSUE that would take Marg below the shelf, or a RECEIVE above it, by more than `stock.voucher_shelf_tol_packs` (1 pack).
  - A held line is the owner's, with its own sentence (`below_zero`, `past_shelf`, `not_ready`).
  - Items it cannot judge against the shelf are counted: a family, no count, goods not yet billed, or a bill keyed after the closing.
- **Darpan's spot points:**
  - judged on the shelf figure for a counted item, on Marg's figure plus taps otherwise;
  - the basis is stored on the point and kept by a correction;
  - a family size's count is written with the plain receipt only — no verdict, no loss, outside the month's leakage — and is the owner's approximate line.
- **The shelf-gap flags:**
  - one set per end day, in `s577_shelf_gap`;
  - written only when the sale and purchase reach covers the closing;
  - its previous figures come from the capture before it.
  - The old `s454_shelf_gap` is kept and read by nothing. Its 22 flags of 18-Oct had been judged on the 09:37 shop-hours capture and were not carried.
- **The Loss desk only reads (F-841).**
  - Its full build writes only its cache.
  - A line that sold more than was counted is marked *"sold after count"* for the owner, never closed by itself.
  - The two 27-09 system closures (DOLOGESIC SP, TYCOB 1500) stand, named.
- ***Stock now* and *drift*** show the two figures of record. The PC's old estimate is shown beside them to the doctor role only, and only for the side-by-side week.
- **The side-by-side week (4b-2's start condition).** `stock_figure.py witness` writes `/root/finance/spine/stock_figure_witness_<weekday>.txt`. Its nightly crontab line is the parent's to add. Then `expected_units`, push_expected and marg_shadow go.
- **Retired:** `sales_test`, `shelf_figure.latest_snapshot`, `_s524_capture` and its cache, `_voucher_held`, `Spine.latest_close_day`, the bare-clip spine keys of the arrival readers.
- **Left, by rung:**
  - the count's own figures — rung 4f;
  - the order engine's — S486 B;
  - `stock_snapshot`'s list readers and writer — rung 5;
  - `stock_spot_check` — rung 4d.

- **The problem.** A supplier's bill, scanned at reception, ties to that supplier's oldest open order and marks the whole order arrived (`order_scan_tie`). The shelf figure added every unanswered line at the ordered quantity. It dropped a line only on a spine purchase from the same supplier, dated on or after the order day, and never expired it (F-930).
- **What it caused on 18-Oct:** 13 counted items stood above Marg — TOLTRIS PLUS +500, CROCAL +300, FLACORT 6 +200, PREGHYPE NT TAB +200, PRETOL 8 +100, THIO Q AP +100, LINVIZ 600 +100, and small ones.

  - the line's own Marg bill;
  - never a bill another order line already claimed;
  - before the order day, only from the order's own vendor, within `arrival.back_days` (2);
  - another supplier only on or after the order day.

### 15N.4 · D740 — the start-of-day closing (the owner's "yes", 16:24 IST) and the morning-flow kit (S587_MORNING_FLOW, live 17:56 IST; read live 18:03)

**The ruling.** *A closing printed for today and taken before the day's first sale counts as the START of that day — the end of yesterday plus the morning's Marg work.* It amends charter rule 11 (§15M.3 A). Put to him in one line on the mock-up page; his answer *"1 yes"*. Nothing changes for Shavez whichever date he prints.

**How the system keeps it (built beside the spine, the spine's own build and gate untouched):**
- **A start capture** is a `sp_close_name` capture printed day D and stamped on D between 09:00 and 22:00. `end_day()` (the clock rule) is unchanged; `captures()` makes such a capture usable for the end of D−1 **only when its verdict is `accepted`** in the new `finance.db` table **`stock_start_capture`** (`md5, as_on, stamp_iso, end_day, verdict pending|accepted|set_aside, reason, checked_at, built, prev_md5, n_keys, n_excluded, n_unexplained, detail`). Two usable captures for one end day: the newest by stamp wins (the morning's Marg work is in the later one).
- **The test (R2-B2, per key by `k20`):** `change = (units_S − bal(k, D−1)) − (units_P − bal(k, P.end_day))`, P the previous verified capture, `bal` the spine's own ledger (`sp_move`, OPENING included). **Excluded by cause** (the morning's known non-spine Marg work): count-voucher batches first entered in (P, S]; `amir_job` `fix:%` taps done in the window; `loss_piles.settings(con)["consume_keys"]`; keys with a PURCHASE dated D; bills with `purchase_bill.first_seen_day` after P's day. An unexcluded drop no larger than that day's SALE units (absolute) = **a sale before the capture → `set_aside`**; more than `count.start_unexplained_max` (3) other unexplained changes → `set_aside`; else **`accepted`**, the unexplained keys `approx` on that capture.
- **When:** only once the spine's `last_sale ≥ D` (the next morning's sale report at the earliest — a start capture is never used on its own day) **and** the purchase side covers S; until then **`pending`, never `set_aside` for a short reach**. Judged lazily on any read with a writable connection and by `python3 stock_figure.py verify-start`; a final verdict is a record (the nightly spine rebuild can move `bal()` under an accepted capture — `built` records which spine judged; a re-judge rule is left for later).
- **The first verdicts (17:50:18 IST):** accepted — 04-10 10:22, 08-10 09:54, 09-10 09:48 (twice), 15-10 10:05 and 10:22; set aside — 01-10 21:39 (an evening print; a sale came before it: CALBERT K27, DOLOGESIC SP, NAPROVIN D, RIFAVAX 550, ROVIC DSR, …); pending — 18-10 09:37 (until the 19-10 sale report and a purchase export after Amir's 09:35 tap). On the walk's copy with a synthetic reach the 18-10 09:37 capture was accepted: 372 keys, 47 excluded by cause (the four `fix:` items among them), no deciding key. **A design point for later:** an evening print is a start-capture candidate by the 09:00–22:00 clause; the sale test sets it aside today; a latest-hour bound would say so earlier.
- **A same-day purchase on a start capture (R2-B3)** is counted once: `marg()` adds no PURCHASE dated D over the start capture; such a key is `approx` (`purchase_on_start`) and skipped by the proof and the shelf-gap flags whether the start capture is the judged one or the base; `early_bill` is folded in and retired.
- **Visit cover (R2-B4): `purchase_cover(con, sp, c, reach_, ctx)`** — the purchase side covers a closing by (a) the spine's reach past its end day (as before), (b) the newest purchase export stamped after it (as before), or **(c)** an export (anyone's) stamped at or after the visit day's **last** step-2 row, with no `amir_bill_answer` reason `self` and no open `amir_self_wait` after it — covering every closing from its stamp to the next visit day's first step-2 row, **on the visit day too**; a later export equal to it in `purchase_export.n_rows` and `grand_amount_p` (the owner's 16-10 13:03 = 15-10's 09:10) proves nothing was entered between and is the cover from its own stamp. `tryable(c, reach_, con=None, ctx=None)`; both callers pass `con` — `stock_app` and **`shelf_figure.record_gaps`** (one cover for the shelf-gap flags and the proof). The real days, replayed by the walk: 15-10's 09:10 export is not cover after its re-taps 10:05 / 10:06; the owner's 16-10 export covers 16 and 17-10; 04-10's 10:10 export covers the 10:22 closing; 18-10's 09:25 / 09:27 exports are not cover for the 09:37 closing (the tap 09:35 came after them).
- **The proof onto `captures()` (R2-C1/C2) and "agrees = right at once" (R2-B5):** `_s524_closings` and `close_of` by printed day are retired; `close_of` is `sp_close_name` by md5; **every** usable closing after the last entry is tried for `ok=True`; `wrong` only when the newest capture (`after[-1]`), inside both reaches, disagrees. 194 of 198 items unchanged old → new on the live copy; the four changed are the corrected items below.
- **The hub's words (F-933, R2-C6):** a key in `wrong` whose item has an `amir_job` `fix:%` row done after the judged capture is **`pending_fix`**: *"ITEM: Amir marked it done dd-mm hh:mm — judged on the next closing taken after it"* (his tap, not a Marg fact); the stage line *"… N items Amir marked done after Marg's closing of dd-mm-yyyy — judged on the next closing taken after it"*. Live at 18:03: KT ROS DT 09:23, FOLITRAX 15 MG TAB 09:24, OSTOVAXL DM 09:24, VINBACTUM DS 09:25. (The first placement at 17:50 fell back to S488's *"waiting for our own figure"* for such lots — undone byte-identically at 17:54, the English added, re-placed 17:56.)
- **Amir's page (D737 D, the approved mock-up):** the step **ids** are unchanged in `amir_step_log`, the routes, `S.done`, the gate and the duty map; the **display order** is `ORDER = (1, 2, 6, 3, 4, 5, 7)` — *1 Punch · 2 Bill entry · 3 Marg sudhar · 4 Export · 5 Check · 6 Bills · 7 Band*; `_banner`, *Step N of 7*, `_next_step`, the POST redirect and the visit summary's *skipped* work on positions. **The one new line:** a step-2 row later than the newest step-3 row (or landed export) → the gate holds the day and a red band *"Purchase report dobara nikaaliye — bill entry export ke baad hui hai"* on the later steps; his step-3 row or a landed export clears it. It rests on his own taps (D712). The owner's `/finance/amir/day` says *re-export due since hh:mm* while it stands.
- **Settings:** `count.start_capture` (on) and `count.start_unexplained_max` (3) on the *Counts & watch* card — visible after the 06:30 job or *Refresh watch* (the installer did not refresh the stored watch; a note for every kit that adds a row).

## 15O · Added at v1.24 — 18 → 19-Oct-2026 (session 328)

### 15O.1 · Step 4 of the plan — the buying model re-read against the live bytes (S328, 20:3x → 21:0x IST 18-Oct)

- The 17 files installed after the 18-Oct 01:35 bundle were rebuilt in scratch by their own kits' builders (S574 → S577 → S583 → S584 → S587) and came out at their pins — the live bytes, not a guess (`03_WORKING_PAPERS\S328\S328_READING_S486.md`).
- `order_rules.py` had not moved since S308's read of 11-Oct; `order.source = marg_sheet`, `order.model` absent — the engine reaches only Darpan's unused tab and the weekly score, so the levels model can be built and scored in shadow. The stock base was still mixed (counted items on `stock_figure.shelf`, uncounted on the raw printed-day closing); the arrivals rule lived in three places. Shape decided: **Kit I the plumbing (S593), Kit II the model (next)**.

## 15Q · Added at v1.26 — S332 (19-Oct-2026, the Sanjeevni chat)

### 15Q.1 · What a pharmacy scan is read for — and who reads it now (D747, S601)

- **The uses, read from the 19-Oct bundle** (`claude/S332_SCAN_READING_ASSESSMENT.md`): every live use on the Sanjeevni side reads the **header** of a scan through `purchase_scan_link` (supplier + number + total) — Amir's "bill baaki" line and the paper beside each Marg bill (`amir_day`), vendor payments, the month's bill register and the packs, reception's "is this supplier's bill scanned" (`order_sheet`, `porders`, `porders_s454`), the owner's Needs-you scan lines. **Darpan's flow reads nothing from a scan.** The stock figure and the arrivals rule read Marg's purchase lines, never the scan (S583). The **item lines** are read only by the S446 check (`purchase_sarvam_check`) and the learnt supplier names (`item_check`, `s454_item_name`) — assessment, not operation; their one future use is Marg importing the scanned bill (Y39).
- **D747 (the owner, 15:41 IST 19-Oct):** Claude fills the pharmacy lane's scans — header and items — as it fills the clinic lanes; **Sarvam is switched OFF by the owner's setting `read.sarvam`** (his scan-app Admin page, card *"Reading the scans — Sarvam"*), its code kept; a handwritten pharmacy challan is read by Claude the same way (a challan stays `kind='Challan'` and invisible to the Sanjeevni register, as S531 intended). The other lanes' handwritten challans are the Scan App chat's.
- **S601 (live 17:29:28 IST 19-Oct):** `asset_register._s550_claude` — the pharmacy "beside" thread deleted, the pharmacy paper takes the clinic lanes' path; `_s601_asked()` is the one test of which papers Claude is sent (not other-document, personal, resume, patient papers); `_sarvam_setting()` the one reader of `read.sarvam`; a paper Claude was asked to read and could not is **retried by the app three times (10 / 30 / 90 min)**, never for an excluded paper or a paper read as empty; **every fill writes a `bill_audit` row `filled {by: claude | sarvam, items}`** — the one record of which reader filled a paper (F-949's remedy); `bill_extract` asks Claude first; `claude_reread.to_fill` includes the pharmacy lane (future re-reads only — the 140 stored readings are applied by nothing, by design).
- **What it costs:** one reading a paper (Claude ~₹1.45) instead of two (~₹1.80 today); 80–100 Marg purchase bills a month. A Sonnet-vs-Haiku shadow trial on next month's bills is the chat's own later work (`read.claude_model` is `claude-sonnet-5-5`; Haiku is the reader's named fall-back).
- **What a reader of the scan pages should know:** at first a few more "which supplier is this?" questions may reach reception's choice — `order_sheet` learns supplier spellings from the filled `vendor` (`purchase_scan_alias.ocr_norm`), and Claude's spellings differ a little from Sarvam's learnt ones; each is learnt once. A `failed` paper is "unread" to `order_sheet.unread()` at once; with the retry its header may arrive up to two hours later.

### 15Q.2 · Darpan's purchase cut-off line (D746 done, S602)

- **On the tab:** one grey line under the heading of *आज का ऑर्डर* — *"Marg में खरीद dd-mm के बिल तक चढ़ी है — उसके बाद आया माल इस सूची के स्टॉक में नहीं है"*. The owner's "Yes" (18:01 IST) to the mock-up `claude.ai/artifact/MH6Y3vS181GvLw9LRhcEkF`. No stock-day line.
- **Where the date comes from:** `spine_read.purchases_upto()` — the newest PURCHASE bill date in `sp_purchase_bill`, never past today; served as `purchase_upto` by `_s485_payload` (the handle from `order_rules._s470_spine()` closed in a `finally`; None when the spine cannot be opened — then no line). **Not** the report's reach: `stock_figure.reach(sp)["last_pur"]` (the exports' `date_to`) and `stock_app._s488_purchase_reach` (`purchase_export.period_to`) are a different fact and stay where they are.
- **Live at install:** the line reads 18-10 (the live API, 19:56 IST). The tab is not in use while `order.source = marg_sheet`.

The witness log `/root/finance/spine/stock_figure_witness.log` starts with the night of 19-10.

## 15R · Added at v1.27 — S333 (20-Oct-2026, the Sanjeevni chat)

### 15R.1 · Amir's flow off the calendar (S603)

- **The export fact.** `amir_day._export_fact(cx, day)` → `(lo, fact, t2)`: the visit's first and last "Sab bill daal diye" taps, and the newest server receipt of a purchase export after the first (VERIFIED `mi_file` rows of the pair's types and `purchase_export` BILLWISE/ITEMWISE/BILLITEMWISE, superseded or not, any day). The re-export band (`_reexport_due`) is due when a bill was entered after the newest receipt; it never shows before the visit's first file; the step-3 tap ("Dono report nikaal di") only starts the six-minute clock (F-952).
- **Step 4** takes the day's export that covers 1st..today before the newest one; a pair that did not come says *"hh:mm baje se nahi aayi"*. A zero-line report is an answer (since S480).
- **The owner's line after 48 hours** (`amir.proof_wait_hours`): for an open visit day of the last seven, *"Amir's purchase export of dd-mm never reached the server …"* or *"… re-export owed (Amir)"* — read from receipts, never from the live set (a past day's pair is superseded by the next export).
- **Purchase cover in words.** `stock_app.readiness` and `stock_watch.start_capture_lines` speak through `stock_figure.purchase_cover_how` on the capture: *"No purchase export has been taken since the closing stock of dd-mm-yyyy was captured …"* / *"Closing stock printed dd-mm hh:mm waits for a purchase export taken after it …"*; nothing on a quiet day (F-769). Both read with `Ctx(read_only=True)` — a GET never judges a start capture (F-953). The stock-now page's `_s577_record` still judges on a GET (by design, R2-B2; named).
- **The waiting reason** "not printed under their name in Marg's closing of D" (`absent`) when every unseen row is `absent_at_try` (F-786).
- **An answered salt** is a line on Amir's SALT KAAM tab (*"Item ka salt badliye — Dr sahab ka jawab"*, the owner's answer the exact text); duty `amir.salt_tasks` (DUTY_MAP v16; door `/finance/amir/step/6`, the `saltsdoor` card).

### 15R.2 · The vendor-payment page and the month lock (D748, S604)

- **The Month-end reports card** on `/finance/purchase/page/pay/<M>`: one line each for the **supplier-wise statement**, the **bill-wise statement** and the **item lines** — *Received dd-Mon hh:mm · period · N bills* (the supplier-wise also *"its total ₹… agrees with the sheet"*), or *"Not yet — export from Marg as a TEXT file: SUPPLIER WISE PURCHASE STATEMENT, FROM 01-MM-YYYY TO <last>-MM-YYYY"* (BILL WISE …, SUPPLIER/ITEM WISE …); item lines with bills still missing name them; a refusal from the medical PC after the month's end is one line under the card (*"nothing for you to redo"* for a reader stop). All three in and agreeing → *"All three reports are in and agree — the month can be locked."* (`_month_reports`, the covering export by the month's last day).
- **The lock** (`_lock_gate`): `_month_summary`'s reasons plus each report not received plus the cross-check (`_verify_statement`, to the month's last day) not agreeing. The lock, the month page's FINALISE, the hub's "ready to finalise" and the card's green line all read it; `_month_summary` and every money reader are unchanged by it. April, May and June (never locked) now wait for their supplier-wise statements; July ready; August FINAL.
- **Returns.** The medical PC's reader (`marg_txt.py` S604, e8a60d9d) accepts a grouped purchase block whose lines exceed its printed TOTAL when a set of ≤6 of its ≤40 positive lines makes exactly half the excess in both columns; the server counts a negative bill's positive lines as a return at read time (`signed`). The supplier totals never reach the server; the bill's sign decides. April (bills 248, 261), May (339), July (64, 68) and September's L.K. returns (5851, 6115, 6708) count as returns.
- **One bill per (number, date, amount) across reports.** `_store_bills` joins a clipped-name twin (one name a word-boundary prefix of the other; the other report's md5 set; re-exports included) and audits it `twin_joined`; Bass 14538's twin merged (`twin_merge 542<-579`, 579 kept, not effective).
- **The boxes.** Carried in on a full keyboard (a minus on a phone); `−`, `–`, a trailing `/-` read; an unreadable figure refused; the note kept; *"Saved dd-Mon hh:mm by <user> — payable now ₹…"* on the editable sheet (not the printed pack). **Paid is a record; Carried in moves the bank file** (−amount takes a vendor paid outside the bank off it).
- **September at the close:** purchases ₹4,31,016 (Marg's own); NEFT ₹4,29,019; cheque ₹1,597 (Bass, no account); Agarwal Carried in −400 / Paid 400; 86 bills, all agree; ready to finalise — the owner locks it.

## 15V · Added at v1.31 — S339, 22-Oct-2026 (evening)

### 15V.1 · The one name list (D761, D762 — S629, `med_names.py`)

- **What it is:** one table, `med_name`, in `finance.db`. **kind `marg`** — two Marg items that are one medicine (at the first pass: **THIO Q AP = THIO Q AP TAB**; **LINVIZ 600 = LINVIZ 600 6T**). **kind `scan`** — a dealer's printed spelling of a Marg item (69 at the first pass, from the 134 pharmacy scans linked to their Marg bills and read by Claude — the September bills were the seed, D762).
- **How it learns, and only when sure:** a Marg pair from an order — the own dealer's bill after the order carries another item whose name differs only by TAB / CAP / STRIP / a tablet count (`6T`), never by a strength, a size (`6*4` vs `8*4`), a letter or a variant word (FORTE, PLUS, DSR, SR …); the price per tablet within 5 % of the item's last three purchases or the order's rate; both packed as strips. A scan spelling — the same bill, quantity and rate agree, the names pass the loose test, one to one. **Anything weaker is asked of the owner once** on his track page (*"X on Deepam's bill A000678 = Y? The same medicine?"* Yes / No); a scan spelling is never asked. A substitute is never sure (D760). It runs at most every 10 minutes (`names.learn_gap_min`), from `porders.detect_supplies`.
- **Who reads it:** the arrival rule (`stock_figure._carried`, through `arrival_forms` — never `printed_forms`, so a stock figure, a count, a closing or a split is per Marg item exactly as before), the carry (*already on Marg's order*, *re-ordered*), the soft note on the track page. `s454_item_name` stays, unread by the order list.

### 15V.2 · The order list made right (S629)

- **Marg's matching:** the own dealer's bill first; a medicine on the bill under its partner name counts; two lines of one item on one bill summed, and a line billed earlier at the first of two lines is raised (never lowered); **when the bill's strip size differs from the order's, the tablets decide** (LINVIZ 600: 6 strips of 6 = 36 tablets of 100 → came 3 strips of 10, short 7, the note *"36 tablets came on the bill (another pack size)"*); a medicine on **another dealer's bill** within 7 days of the order reads *came from <dealer> (bill n)* — never short, never carried (it still counts for stock only, D743).
- **The carries follow:** a line no longer short drops its carry (*came after all*); a carry follows its line's short (LINVIZ 10 → 7).
- **The owner's track page** (`https://followup.dr-manoj.in/finance/porders/s454/track`, Shavez's in Roman Hindi): "came X of Y"; *arrived — nothing on it came*; **Send back for rescan** on every scan an order card names (or *sent back ✓ — Withdraw*), and Withdraw on the asks; *Change dealer to <dealer>* on *Saved under another dealer* (the owner and Shavez; the dealer read by the server from the paper; the S440 matcher's door too; the tie moves to that dealer's untied order of the week before, else is removed); *The same paper scanned twice*; the clearer scan of a duplicate family read for the notes. **The scans page** (`https://followup.dr-manoj.in/finance/porders/s454/scans`): English only for the owner; *Every pharmacy scan · last 14 days* folded, each with Send back.
- **A set-aside scan** (S631): never offered Send back, never named twice; when a card's clearer copy is set aside, the tied scan is offered instead.

### 15V.3 · A pack change in Marg (D763)

- Marg fixes one strip size per item (1 strip = N tablets), and the rate and every sale follow it. **Never change the strip size of an item that still has stock** — the rate of the stock on the shelf would be disturbed.
- **The new pack is a new item** with the pack in its name (`LINVIZ 600 6T`, 1*6); every new purchase goes into it. **The old item's stock is sold first** — Darpan hands over and bills the old item until it is zero (the bill names the strip handed over). **Then the old item is retired with the ZZ prefix** (as `ZZLINVIZ 600` was in September). The one name list joins the two, so ordering and arrivals carry straight across.
- **Open (F-1000):** a shortfall and its carry are still counted in the order's strip size (LINVIZ's carry reads 7 strips of 10; Kedar sends strips of 6 — 64 tablets = 11 strips of 6; reception corrects it on the card). Proposed: count in tablets, the strip size from the dealer's latest bill, one line on Amir's page when a pack changes — the owner's yes awaited.

## 15W · Added at v1.32 — S340, 23-Oct-2026 (early morning)

*No live file changed. This section records what the system holds about batches and expiry, what Marg's monthly reports are and whether the system reads them, the four exports the owner made on 23-Oct and what they showed, and the flow he settled for expiry, near expiry, wrong-batch sales and dump stock (D771) and the monthly exports (D772). F-1000 (the pack-change build) was declined by him at 04:07.*

### 15W.1 · Marg's monthly reports — what the system reads as text (the owner's note, points 1–4)

| report (Marg's name) | read as text today? | where it goes |
|---|---|---|
| Stock Expiry — expired / near expiry (*EXP. BEFORE*) | **yes** (STOCK_EXPIRY; the last before 23-Oct: 05-Oct, 266 lines) | the archive; `spine/near_expiry.py` nightly into `spine/expiry/` — on no screen |
| Dump stock, batch-wise (*WHOLE STORES CLOSING STOCK* with Batch / Expiry) | **no** — refused at the medical PC (*a closing stock the reader cannot take*), kept | — |
| Not sold, last N days (*WHOLE STORES CLOSING STOCK*, item-wise) | taken — **as the day's closing stock** (F-1008); kept out of the stock figure only by the subset and 250-item rules | the archive |
| Batch Stock, whole store (*WHOLE STORES CLOSING STOCK AS ON …* with Batch / Expiry) | **no** — refused, kept | — (the D771 build reads it) |
| List of items (*LIST OF ITEMS*) | **yes** (ITEM_MASTER; 05-Oct) | the archive |
| Salt-wise item list | **yes** (SALT_WISE_ITEM_LIST; 11-Oct) | the salt pages |
| Tally export | not a report — Marg's data file for Tally | the accountants' pack (the parent's, D770) |

### 15W.2 · What the system already holds about batches and expiry

- **Every sale line** carries its batch and expiry: `finance.db` `sale_line_item` (22,686 lines since 01-Apr) and `mi_sale_line`; the spine's `sp_sale_line`.
- **Every purchase line** carries its batch and expiry: `finance.db` `purchase_line` (4,214 lines). The spine's `sp_purchase_line` does not.
- **No store holds stock by batch.** The day's closing (`sp_close`) is per item. Only Marg's batch-wise reports say how many strips of each batch are on the shelf — so Marg's export is the one input; between two exports the daily sales name new wrong-batch sales.
- **Marg's own rule (the owner, 04:17):** Marg stops selling a batch in its expiry month. A batch of 11/2026 is unsellable from 1-Nov. `near_expiry.py` counts to the printed expiry (F-1009).
- **Marg's "not sold" list counts any movement** — a sale, a purchase or a stock voucher. On 23-Oct it named 26 items; the system's own sales name 77 in stock with no sale since 23-Aug, all 26 among them; 30 of the 51 Marg omits had an October count voucher.

### 15W.3 · The four exports of 23-Oct — what they showed

| export (captured) | rows · items | read |
|---|---|---|
| Near expiry, 04:21:53 | 16 batches, TOTAL 1,217 | blocked from 1-Nov: REEBORN D 0:5, RIFAVAX 550 0:5, VOM L 3:2; 12/2026 INTACOXIA-60 57:10, DOLOGESIC SP 18:2 |
| Dump stock batch-wise, 04:29:48 / 04:30:05 | 147 · 122 | 58 of the 122 sold in the last 30 days — a filtered stock list, not an unsold one |
| Not sold 60 days, 04:43:01 | 26 · 26 (66 units) | 18 orthotics; BONMAX PTH PEN, HYORTH XL, PROTICO AM ICE, FELBATE, HADJOD 60, HISTOCALAMIN LOTION; CARTILOX SACHET and VINBACTUM DS expired |
| **Batch Stock, whole store, 05:10:25 / 05:10:48** (as on 22-10) | 362 · 239 (88 with several batches) | **blocked now** 4 batches (CARTILOX SACHET 3, EXLAIN A 1:2, RUNVACE TP 1:0, VINBACTUM DS 25 — 2/2025); **blocked from** 1-Nov 3, 1-Dec 7 (INTACOXIA-60 865 tablets), 1-Jan 4, 1-Feb 7; **36 negative batches in 30 medicines**, 12 sold against no batch; GLI-ME SR1 and NORTIMER below zero overall; **dump 76** (36 orthotics, 40 medicines; 17 of the medicines also expire by 4/2027) |

The four files are kept in `D:\Downloads\ClaudeCowork\03_WORKING_PAPERS\S340\samples\` (md5 `001dcc68…` near expiry, `942d8664…` dump, `104bd3c7…` not sold, `2aab62ce…` batch stock). They carry the shop's licence and phone in the head — never into the repository (F-185).

### 15W.4 · The expiry, near-expiry, wrong-batch and dump flow (D771) — settled 23-Oct, not built

```
 Amir, first visit of the month (Shavez the backup)
   Marg: Stocks -> Batch Stock -> WHOLE -> Available -> A Stock Statement -> B Alphabatic -> View -> Save
        |
        v   one text file, filed under its OWN name (never STOCK_CLOSING; read by nothing that reads stock)
 the nightly list  +  the daily sales (already held)
   1  blocked now ............ Amir: stock issue voucher -> breakage / expiry ledger (or the dealer's return)
   2  near expiry ............ counted to the month BEFORE the printed expiry
                               -> the owner prescribes those brands; Darpan sells that batch first
   3  wrong-batch sales ...... a batch below zero beside one with stock; a sale with no batch
                               -> Amir corrects in Marg monthly; new ones named the next day
   4  dump ................... in stock, no sale and no purchase in 60 days (the system's own sales)
                               -> the owner: return, push, or stop re-ordering
        |
        v
 the owner's English page (all four) · one do-this line on Darpan's tab · one on Amir's page
```

- **One input, one list, one build; read-only.** The build changes no stock figure, order list, purchase record or morning check; its test proves every item's stock figure unchanged with the report present. The day's item-wise closing stays the only stock input.
- **The same separation covers F-1008:** the not-sold list and the batch-wise shapes are recognised by their columns and size, not their title.
- **Fallback:** if Marg cannot print the whole store, the Stock Expiry export (already read) serves sections 1 and 2.
- **Stays manual by design:** the write-off voucher and the batch corrections in Marg (Amir).
- **Sequence:** the mock-up (the owner's page, Darpan's line, Amir's line, the how-to card) → his yes → one kit, tested on the four real files of 23-Oct → his one install line.

### 15W.5 · The monthly exports and their how-to (D772)

## 15Z · Added at v1.35 — S350, 25-Oct-2026

### 15Z.1 · The buying model, live behind one setting (D678 built, D785 — S644_BUYING_MODEL, live 08:22 IST 25-Oct)

The ordering engine (§6 of the sections; Kit I S593 put it on the one stock function) now makes its list in one of two ways, by **`order.model`** — on the owner's approvals page, *Buying rules for medicines* → *How the list is made*, one line and one button:

- **fixed** — today's engine, line for line: each supplier's calendar (weekly Monday / fortnightly / monthly / Kedar's Monday + Friday), the rate as sales of the last 28 days to today ÷ 28, the box rounding then 10-then-tens, a between-review order when cover falls under lead + safety. Proved equal to the pre-S644 bytes on three days by the kit's walk; one button away.
- **levels** (live since the install; `order.source = marg_sheet` read and untouched, so **no staff screen changed**): **(1) each supplier at its own rhythm** — `rhythm_mode learnt`: due when today − last buy ≥ its interval, the median gap of its bill days over 90 days (the spine's bills, `purchases_by_supplier`), half-up, clamped `order.review_min_days` 2 … `order.review_max_days` 30, the default 7 under `order.min_bills_for_rhythm` bill days, the owner's `review_days` overriding; `rhythm_mode fixed` (Kedar, and any row with owner-set days or a custom cadence — seeded once at the columns' creation) keeps the calendar. *Last buy* = a sent or received purchase order that is not interim-only, or a sent fixed/darpan proposal; the newest bill day only when neither is within 30 days; strictly before the day. **(2) the shop's own lots** — the median purchase units of `order.lot_history_days` (180), rounded up to whole lots, the cover cap as a limit (never below one lot — `confirm` and a line when one lot is more than the cap); a medicine first bought within `order.new_item_days` (28) gets one lot; **a lot-based line is not rounded to tens**; an item with no lot keeps today's rails. **(3) the rate ends on the spine's last sale day, never today** — long `order.pace_days` 28, short `order.rate_short_days` 7, the short rate counted when the item sold on `order.rate_short_min_sell_days` 3 of them and capped at `order.rate_short_cap_pct` 200 of the long. **(4) the shelf keeps one large sale** — the level is never below the largest single day's sale of `order.big_day_days` 28 (0 = off); a line `plan_line` left out is listed when the shelf is under that day's sale. **(5) between reviews** an order goes only when stock ÷ rate < days to the next review + lead + `order.interim_margin_days` 1 (today's engine added the full safety), sized to the horizon with safety through the lot. The cover cap follows the interval (≤ 7 weekly, ≤ 14 fortnightly, else monthly) unless the owner set the row's cap. A sent order's unanswered lines count as stock within max(`order.on_order_days`, interval + lead) of its own supplier.

**The list made again (B.2a; S308 v).** `prepare_day` (09:00) stamps what the list stood on — the sales day, the closing's day, the spine's build — in `order_notice` (slot `made_through`). Every ten-minute tick before `order.relist_until` (13:00), when any of the three moved and nobody touched the list (every row `open` / `held`, no darpan row, no Darpan edit, no line edit, no call, no print, no WhatsApp), the list is made again once: rows UPDATED in place by (day, supplier, kind), ids kept, nothing deleted; a touched list with moved inputs is marked `stale` for the owner's fold. On `fixed` nothing of this runs.

**The arithmetic is pure** — eight functions in `order_rules.py` (`model_rate`, `model_big_day`, `model_lot`, `model_interval`, `model_cap_days`, `model_level`, `model_qty`, `model_interim_due`), no database, no clock, unit-tested in `selftest_spine.py` (`test_s644`, 20 checks) and run by the kit's replay (`replay_s644.py`) over the shop's months on the nightly copy's tables: **0.0 % from the S295 reference** (04-May … 22-Oct: lost sale 0.69 %, 84 empty-shelf item-days against the shop's 231, Rs 1.70 L average stock, 20.4 supplier orders a week). On the 19 days the model never saw (04 … 22-Oct): shop 27 · engine 26 · model 10, at 18.8 orders a week against Darpan's 15.1.

**The score (B.8).** The nightly rehearsal (`order_rehearsal.py`, 23:58) keeps `model`, `held`, `added`, `qty_engine` and `made_through` on every line; a line Darpan added is not scored, a held line is scored as listed, the engine's quantity is the one scored; the weekly file carries `by_model` once a week holds two models and `darpan` (held / added / changed). The owner's line reads the per-model figures when present. **The first `levels` week is scored on the night of 1-Nov**; the 14-day figure a week later. **D673's bar** — three weeks at 85 % / 80 % / no more stock-outs than Darpan's sheet — brings the switch (K4, `order.source = system`) to the owner for his word; never before, never by itself (D785).

**The owner's door.** `api/rules/state` carries `model` (value, words, the other value, the eleven settings, the inputs of the last plan, the day's stamps, each supplier's rhythm — mode, interval, bill days, last buy, next, cap, the score line); the approvals fold draws it (`poModelHTML`); the supplier form gains *Rhythm* (learnt | fixed) and *Review every N days*; the old page's card (`?old=1`) carries the same one line and button. Every setting is validated and audited through `/finance/porders/api/s454/setting` (a word, a clock, the min ≤ max cross-check).

**Not built, by the charter (S308):** B.6 *count it* (`shelf_figure` already judges Marg against the shelf once); B.5 on Darpan's tab (the not-known list stays on the owner's fold — a medicine never bought has no supplier and its first bill names it); Darpan's tab header line (with the kit that moves the source). Nothing of `why`, the rates or the lots reaches a staff screen.

His publish `61685e2a` (07:43:43 IST); the 07:44 server line refused by the install window (08:02–23:49), nothing written; the install **DONE 08:22 IST** — the installer repeated the build, the walk and the replay on the box's own bytes, placed six files with their `.bak_S644_<from8>` backups and `finance.db.bak_S644_<stamp>`, restarted `clinic-finance` once, ran the data step (four columns, eleven settings, `order.model = levels` because `order.source = marg_sheet`) and printed what the staff see next: nothing.

### 15AA.2 · What the spine's "EXCEPT N items" means (F-1025)

The status line *stock = Marg at <day> EXCEPT N item(s)* counts every item where Marg's closing differs from the spine's carry at the latest checkable closing. Since 16-Oct it has read 189–192 with the same figures every night: **count #1's medicine stock vouchers** that Amir keyed on 15-Oct (30 batches, 170 items). The spine is built only from Marg's exports and reads no voucher, so its carry differs from Marg by exactly those changes. **Marg is right.** Ordering is unaffected (`order.stock_basis = count`: the shelf figure starts from the 6-Sep count, which the vouchers brought Marg to). The repair (after 1-Nov) classes such a finding VOUCHER; vouchers are never added to `sp_move`.

- **D796 — the rest of the S335 list:** closed without a build — `_wa_text` (the door is shut), one scan per order (`order_track` and `order_sheet` take every scan of the dealer in the window), BILL/ITEM WISE's returns (that export not taken since 05-Oct; ITEMWISE replaced it), the 09-01..10-01 bill-wise export (no `purchase_bill` reads it), the learnt item names (since D747 Claude's reading fills `bill_items`, which `item_check` learns from); parked until the buying switch — F-984's stock readers, one `newest_stamps()` door, `_s577_record` (all under the shelf figure); B-0167 one staff tap; the Sonnet-vs-Haiku trial the Scan App chat's (`assetapp/claude_reader.py`).

### 15AB.2 · The expiry watch made current and right (S665, S668)

- **Made in the day.** `spine/near_expiry.py --if-newer`: the watch is made only when the spine's `MAX(sp_sale_line.date)` is newer than the newest run's `sales_to` (or no run exists); otherwise silent. Crontab: `5-59/10 9-23 * * *`, under `flock -n /tmp/spine.lock` (the spine's own build is `*/10 8-23` under the same lock). The 23:57 line unchanged. Normally one daytime run a morning, ten minutes after Shavez's export reaches the spine (F-1033).
- **The start of a day (D740).** `expiry_watch._sales_after(R)`: the day whose END a Batch Stock stands for, by `stock_figure.end_day` on the stamp in the file's own name — a same-day print before 09:00 = the day before; after 22:00 or for an earlier day = its own day; in shop hours = its own day (as before). `compute()` takes off the sales, reads *bought since* and runs the written-off check from that day (F-1034). VOM L C1235 (3 strips, sold on 26-Oct) left the list at 14:57 IST 27-Oct.

### 15AB.3 · Sections in the dump (S669, S671; D795)

- **Across Marg's widths.** Marg prints one item at different widths in different reports (the closing stock 28 letters, the Batch Stock 30, a voucher 26). `expiry_watch.ortho_of` looks up `stock_item_section` by exact key, then `_twin()`: the ONE table key that is a prefix of the name or that the name is a prefix of, the shorter at least `TWIN_MIN = 12` letters; two candidates = none. On the 26-Oct Batch Stock a twin is used for 8 names, every one a width-spelling of the same item (F-1035).
- **The owner's rows.** CAST SHOE S TYNOR and PRIME CAST SHOE S → Orthotics (S669; the seed had CAST as a consumable). The nine crepe bandages (CREPE 8CM 3IN SUNLINE, CREPE BANDAGE 6CM 2IN, GLOCREPE, LEUKOCREPE 6 / 8 / 10CM 4IN / 15CM 6INC, TOPCREPE 10 / 15 CM) → Medicines (S671, **D795**: pharmacy sale stock, not consumables); LEUKOBAND 4 INCH has been Medicines since 18-Sep. Dump 24 + 33 → 27 + 30.
- **The dump is per item, not per family.** The five sizes he named as "regularly sold" had no sale in 60 days (their sister sizes did); the dump is right to list them.
- **Residue:** the S268 word lists still class CREPE and CAST SHOE as consumables for a name the table does not hold (a size Marg adds later). The next section kit.

## 15AC · Added at v1.38 — S361, 28-Oct-2026

### 15AC.1 · The home / procedure medicine deduction, made where the bills land (S675; D798; F-1037)

- **The rows.** A Marg bill whose customer text carries Darpan's label words (`setting` `noncash.home_words` "HOME MEDI"; `noncash.proc_words` "PROSIJ, PROSEJ, PROCIJ, PROCED, PROSED, PRUSIJ") is not cash in the drawer: it becomes one `day_noncash_bill` row (`home_medicine` / `procedure_medicine`), which `v_cash_ledger`, the day panel and Darpan's *Kal ka hisaab* (`darpan_kal.compute_day` — `home_p`, `proc_p`) subtract. The bill is found where the ingest leaves it: the review queue (a name with no ID or phone, confidence 0.5), `identity_resolution` / `identity_dispute` (laddered or name-checked, S399), or `sale_item.home_med = 1`.
- **Before S675** only `day_resync.py` pass 2 (`*/30 7-23`) wrote those rows, and only on an UNAPPROVED day. A day approved between the export's arrival and the next run kept its bill undeducted for good (pass 2 only names it in its log): A004474 of 27-Oct (Rs 98) — export ~08:31, approved before 09:00.
- **Since S675** `finance_ingest.ingest_day` calls `day_resync.at_ingest(con, unit, business_date)` at its end, in the ingest's own transaction, under `SAVEPOINT s672_noncash` (any error rolls back to the savepoint; the ingest stands as before). `at_ingest` acts only for the medical unit, a day `submitted` / `draft`, since 2026-09-01, the Sanjeevni switch not OFF; it runs pass 2's own `noncash_day(..., apply=True, commit=False, via="S672 at ingest")` (rows + one `audit_log` `noncash_sync`). A caller's rollback (S474, the push path's row-count check) takes the rows with it. Pass 2 stays, and now reads PRESENT. **An approved day is never touched by either** — a gap there is corrected visibly, as 27-Oct was.
- **27-Oct.** The one-time step (`fix_s672.py`, refusing unless A004474 was the day's only undeducted label bill) added the row and re-decided Darpan's 27-Oct handover (`darpan_kal._decide`): handed Rs 8,380 to Dr Bhawna (entered 08:42 by manoj), expected Rs 8,477 → Rs 8,379, diff +1, complete, confirmed.

S672's installer looked for the 27-Oct export there and stopped at [4/8] (F-1038). The proof with the real export runs on manojz (`walk_s675.py --export`, 14 of 14); on the box the walk ingests made-up bills into a made-up day on a copy of the live database and runs the one-time step on a copy of the live 27-Oct.

## 15AD · Added at v1.39 — S362, 28-Oct-2026

### 15AD.1 · Sections that follow the owner's rulings (S676; D799; F-1035's residue)

- **`stock_item_section` is seeded on every closing.** `stock_app.api_snapshot` calls `section_map.on_closing` after a Marg closing (`push_snapshot`) is stored — never for the computed feed (`push_expected`); wholly inside a try (a fault never refuses a closing). A name with no row gets one; a row is never overwritten.
- **The family rule.** `section_map.family(name)` = the name (`norm_key`) cut at its first size word (S, M, L, XL …, UNIVERSAL, CHILD …) or number word that comes after a product word of three letters or more: CAST SHOE L UM → CAST SHOE; LEUKOCREPE 10CM*4IN → LEUKOCREPE; L S BELT XL … → L S BELT; G DRESS 10 → G DRESS; GLOCREPE → itself. A family whose `source='owner'` rows are all one section decides every non-owner sister (`source='family'`, `by_user` names the teaching row, `seeded_as` keeps the word rule's answer); two sections in one family decide nothing. Applied at seeding, at his tap (`set_section` sweeps the sisters and says how many) and once by the installer.
- **At install (13:05):** the 26-Oct closing's 24 unsectioned names seeded; CAST SHOE L TYNOR, CAST SHOE L UM, CAST SHOE M TYNOR → Orthotics (as CAST SHOE S TYNOR). The S268 word lists in `stock_app.py` are count #1's record and are untouched; they retire at rung 5.

### 15AD.2 · The returns engine on the spine (S677; D799; F-1043) — rung 4d, stage 1

- **The door.** `spine_read`: `credit_notes(d1, d2)` (`sp_sale_bill.credit_note = 1`), `return_days(d1, d2)`, `sale_lines(bill, date=None)` (by date and bill; by bill alone, newest date first), `lines_of_bills(bills, k20, returns, date_from, date_to)` (chunked IN, newest first). Read-only.
- **The engine (`finance_returns_audit` v3).** A return is the spine's credit note and its lines, found by date and bill number, or by bill number alone (the spine's date named); nothing from digits or same-day amounts — the S212 ladder retired. The PATIENT stays `sale_item`'s (`patient_ref_id`, the only place the export's patient column lands). His earlier purchases: his bills of the last 180 days from `sale_item`, their lines from the spine by the item's key; the return's own pack first; within one day the larger sale first. One spine door per day, passed down; a spine that cannot be read at that moment makes the return *not examinable* — never NEVER BOUGHT. The row shape is the old one: `item_key` = the normalised name (Marg's sale report prints 20 letters), `amount_p` = the rate per pack, `expiry_ym` from the printed m/yy; the spine key rides as `k20`. `return_days(con, unit, d1, d2)` = the spine's credit-note days ∪ `sale_item`'s return days — one place for `returns_kinds.month_notes`, `finance_returns_escalate` (both functions), `sanjeevni_approvals`' fallback count, `returns_for_range`.
- **Proven on the box at install (15:50):** every credit note since 1-Sep under the old and the new engine on a copy of the database and of the live spine — 41/41 days, 85/85 identical (verdict, money, population, line count, gross, unreadable lines, every line's fields). For the record since 1-Apr: 364 of 377 identical; 5 May credit notes only the spine holds; CN00139 (25-Jul) now *ok* by its own pack; all before `returns.act_from` (2-Sep).
- **Not moved:** Darpan's Vaapsi desk (`returns_desk.py`) — stage 2, with a key migration of `return_line.item_key`; `finance_returns.py`'s lookups (no caller, rung 5); discount attribution (`sale_attribution.py`) — read by nothing, retired at rung 5 (D799).

## 15AE · Added at v1.40 — S367 (29-Oct-2026, the Sanjeevni chat)

### 15AE.1 · Amir's step 4 trusts the server's own door — S678 (F-1045)

- **The fault.** `amir_day._export_state` read only `purchase_export`, which manojz's ten-minute pull and push fill. The medical PC pushes every export to the server's door (`mi_file`) within seconds; manojz was asleep 00:22 – 09:43 on 29-Oct, so Amir's 09:07 pair (VERIFIED at the door) read *"nahi aayi"* after six minutes, and step 4 told him to close Excel and export again.
- **The rule (S678, `amir_day.py` a0907b74).** `_door_receipt(cx, kind, day, first)`: the newest VERIFIED `mi_file` of type `PURCHASE_<kind>` Marg-stamped on the day, non-empty dates covering the 1st..day, received by the server no earlier than his newest step-2 tap. **Today only:** with no `purchase_export` row of the day (or only an older non-covering one), the report is a *wait* carrying the door's time — never *bad*. `_step4`: the line *"Server par hh:mm baje aa gayi — padhi ja rahi hai"*; when every wait is a door wait and nothing is bad or not taken, the head *"Report server par aa gayi hai … Aapko dobara nahi nikaalni"* with no minutes. A door wait never hides a bad or not-taken report (those keep their head and *Dobara banaiye*). **Never a tick from the door alone** — the tick and the bill list still need `purchase_export`'s rows. A past day keeps its own reading.
- **Proven:** walk 22/22 on the 29-Oct copy; old = new on every real day since 1-Sep (24 days, 48 report-days); control 6 red. Two cold-review rounds (one blocker and three corrections built). Live 11:59 IST 29-Oct.
- **Not cured here:** the bill list still waits on manojz. The lasting cure — the server loading the purchase lines from its own door file — is plan step 5's *the second parser + the manojz senders*.

### 15AE.2 · A sale billed to a party is refused by name — S679 (D802, F-1046)

- **The ruling (D802):** a sale is Cash or UPI only; a sale billed to a party account is a wrong entry, corrected in Marg before the export is taken.
- **The 28-Oct case.** A counter stand-in billed A004482 (₹1,198, cash 0) to DEEPAM PHARM; Marg's text prints the party in the D.R. column. The reader refused at line 12 with the generic *"payment head is not where the header says"*; the note became a reader stop (S556) and Shavez's tile said *"aapko kuch nahi karna"*.
- **The rule.** Medical PC `marg_txt.py` (aa7baee5, VERSION S679): a bill row whose D.R. holds a word with a space on its left that is not a payment head (`.x`, blank, `#`, CASH or UPI however printed) refuses *"line N: bill <no> is billed to the party account <name>, not cash or UPI — correct the bill in Marg (Cash or UPI), then export again"*. `marg_watch.py` (a009d2fd): the note carries *"bill <no> is billed to a party account, not cash or UPI"* — the bill number leaves the PC, the party's name never. Server `reports_tile.py` (03902a5d): `hindi_reason` → *"Bill <no> party ke khaate mein bana hai — bikri sirf Cash ya UPI mein hoti hai. Marg mein yeh bill Cash ya UPI mein theek kijiye, phir report dobara nikaaliye."*; not a reader stop, not matched by DUTY_MAP's GLOB — the sale duty stays due.
- **Proven:** the PC walk over all 155 kept texts — 153 the same bytes or refusal, the 2 copies of the 28-Oct text now named; 47 kept reasons give the old note; the watcher's selftest 72 OK. Server walk 7/7, old = new on 25 stored reasons. Live: medical PC 17:30 (the 28-Oct text re-read 17:30:06 naming A004482), server 18:12.
- **Open (F-1047):** a text already refused and noted is not noted again when the reader's reason changes — the server keeps the old reader-stop row for 28-Oct; the next medical-PC kit.
